`ml/run_all.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
  Regenerates every ML result of the project from the raw sessions (one command).

.EXAMPLE
  .\ml\run_all.ps1
  .\ml\run_all.ps1 -RawDir C:\proyecto__ciber\ml\data\raw -WithCnn

  -RawDir    folder with <participant>\{clicks,moves,breaks,sessions}.csv (default: ml\data\raw)
  -WithCnn   also install the optional PyTorch CPU wheel (1D-CNN baseline)
  -SkipJava  do not rebuild/test the plugin after exporting the ONNX model
#>
param(
    [string]$RawDir = (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "data\raw"),
    [switch]$WithCnn,
    [switch]$SkipJava
)
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
Set-Location $PSScriptRoot

function Step($name, [scriptblock]$block) {
    Write-Host "`n=== $name ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    # Python logs to stderr; in Windows PowerShell 5.1 that is not an error. Fail on exit code only.
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"
    & $block 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { "$_" }
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "Step failed: $name (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }
}

$py = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ".venv\Scripts\python.exe"
if (-not (Test-Path $py)) {
    Step "create venv" { python -m venv .venv }
}
Step "install requirements (exact lock)" { & $py -m pip install -q -r requirements-lock.txt }
if ($WithCnn) {
    Step "install torch (CPU)" { & $py -m pip install -q -r requirements-cnn.txt --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu }
}

$env:ML_RAW_DIR = (Resolve-Path $RawDir -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
if (-not $env:ML_RAW_DIR) { Write-Warning "Raw data folder $RawDir not found: only synthetic (DEMO) results will be produced."; $env:ML_RAW_DIR = $RawDir }
$env:PYTHONHASHSEED = "0"

Step "unit tests"                   { & $py -m pytest -q }
Step "A data layer (real data)"     { & $py -m src.data }
Step "B synthetic data"             { & $py -m src.synthetic }
Step "C features"                   { & $py -m src.features }
Step "D models + evaluation"        { & $py -m src.evaluate }
Step "E adversarial experiment"     { & $py -m src.adversarial }
Step "F simulated lag"              { & $py -m src.lag }
Step "G explainability"             { & $py -m src.explain }
Step "H export ONNX + Java fixtures" { & $py -m src.export_onnx }

if (-not $SkipJava) {
    Push-Location (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..")
    try {
        Step "plugin build + Java parity tests" { & .\gradlew.bat build --console=plain }
    } finally { Pop-Location }
}
Write-Host "`nDone. Results in ml\results\ (figures, tables, phase_*_results.md, data_report.md)." -ForegroundColor Green

```

`ml/src/adversarial.py`:

```py
"""Phase E - adversarial experiment (DEMO: synthetic humans and synthetic adversaries).

Round 0  detectors trained on humans + auto_fixed (level "none") only; evaluated on every level.
         The "optimized" adversary is evolved against the round-0 XGBoost under a minimum click
         rate (MIN_CPS within bouts), i.e. it is adaptive to that specific detector.
Round 1  adversarial training: humans + none, uniform, gaussian, human_like, optimized.
         Re-evaluated on every level PLUS "optimized_r2": a fresh adversary evolved against the
         round-1 XGBoost (otherwise round 1 would be tested on the attack it was trained on).
Checks   human FPR round 0 vs round 1 (same calibration protocol), and an attacker utility
         frontier: recall at 1% FPR vs the minimum click rate the optimizer must keep.

    python -m src.adversarial
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as C
from .adversary import generate_attackers, optimize_attack
from .data import build_click_streams
from .evaluate import (TARGETS, fit_final, md_table, prepare_windows, run_cv, summarize,
                       synthetic_windows)
from .synthetic import calibration_from_real, concat_ds

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
MODELS = ["baseline_orig_raw", "baseline_orig_clean", "cv_rule", "iforest", "ocsvm", "rf", "xgb"]
LEVELS = ["none", "uniform", "gaussian", "human_like", "optimized"]
MIN_CPS = 12.0
FRONTIER_CPS = [8.0, 10.0, 12.0, 14.0, 16.0]
N_OPT_ATTACKERS = 24


def windows_from_parts(parts) -> pd.DataFrame:
    ds = concat_ds(parts)
    return prepare_windows(build_click_streams(ds), ds.clicks, "count")


def make_score_fn(det):
    def score_fn(clicks, breaks, sessions):
        sessions = sessions.copy(); sessions["origin"] = "synthetic"
        w = windows_from_parts([(clicks, breaks, sessions)])
        return float(np.mean(det.score(w))) if len(w) else 0.0
    return score_fn


def optimized_windows(det, min_cps: float, rng, net, hyper, level_name: str, n=N_OPT_ATTACKERS,
                      generations=12):
    params, hist = optimize_attack(make_score_fn(det), min_cps, rng, generations=generations, net=net)
    (parts, _) = generate_attackers("optimized", n, rng, net, hyper, opt=params,
                                    prefix=f"SA{level_name}")
    parts[2]["level"] = level_name
    w = windows_from_parts([parts])
    w["level"] = level_name
    return w, params, hist


def plot_main(summ: dict[str, pd.DataFrame], levels_by_round: dict[str, list[str]], path):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, len(summ), figsize=(6.2 * len(summ), 4), sharey=True)
    for ax, (rname, s) in zip(np.atleast_1d(axes), summ.items()):
        lv = levels_by_round[rname]
        x = np.arange(len(lv))
        for _, r in s.iterrows():
            v = np.array([r.get(f"recall@0.01[{l}]", np.nan) for l in lv], float)
            lo = np.array([r.get(f"recall@0.01[{l}]_lo", np.nan) for l in lv], float)
            hi = np.array([r.get(f"recall@0.01[{l}]_hi", np.nan) for l in lv], float)
            fixed = r["model"].startswith("baseline")
            lab = (f"{r['model']} (fixed rule, human FPR {r['fpr@0.01']:.0%})" if fixed
                   else f"{r['model']} (human FPR {r['fpr@0.01']:.1%})")
            ax.errorbar(x, v, yerr=[v - lo, hi - v], marker="o", ms=4, capsize=3,
                        ls="--" if fixed else "-", lw=1.2, label=lab)
        ax.set_xticks(x, lv, rotation=20, fontsize=8)
        ax.set_title(rname, fontsize=9)
        ax.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.02)
        ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    np.atleast_1d(axes)[0].set_ylabel("recall at 1% FPR (95% participant-bootstrap CI)")
    np.atleast_1d(axes)[-1].legend(fontsize=7, loc="lower left")
    fig.suptitle(f"Recall vs adversary level - {C.DEMO_TAG}", fontsize=10)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=140)
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_frontier(fr: pd.DataFrame, path):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.6))
    for (rnd, m), g in fr.groupby(["round", "model"]):
        ax.plot(g["min_cps"], g["recall@0.01"], marker="o", label=f"{m} ({rnd})")
    ax.set(xlabel="minimum click rate imposed on the optimizer (CPS within bouts)",
           ylabel="recall at 1% FPR", ylim=(-0.02, 1.02))
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    ax.legend(fontsize=7)
    ax.set_title(f"Attacker utility vs detectability\n{C.DEMO_TAG}", fontsize=8)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=140)
    plt.close(fig)


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(C.SEED + 1)
    net, hyper, _ = calibration_from_real()
    win = synthetic_windows("count", ["none", "uniform", "gaussian", "human_like"])

    # ---------------- round 0: train on auto_fixed only
    final0 = fit_final(win, ["xgb"], ["none"])
    w_opt, p_opt, h_opt = optimized_windows(final0["xgb"][0], MIN_CPS, rng, net, hyper, "optimized")
    win = pd.concat([win, w_opt], ignore_index=True)
    oof0 = run_cv(win, MODELS, ["none"], LEVELS)
    s0 = summarize(oof0, levels=LEVELS)

    # ---------------- round 1: adversarial training with the harder levels
    final1 = fit_final(win, ["xgb"], LEVELS)
    w_opt2, p_opt2, h_opt2 = optimized_windows(final1["xgb"][0], MIN_CPS, rng, net, hyper, "optimized_r2")
    win1 = pd.concat([win, w_opt2], ignore_index=True)
    levels1 = LEVELS + ["optimized_r2"]
    oof1 = run_cv(win1, MODELS, LEVELS, levels1)
    s1 = summarize(oof1, levels=levels1)

    # ---------------- utility frontier (xgb, both rounds)
    fr_rows = []
    for rnd, fin, train_lv in (("round0", final0, ["none"]), ("round1", final1, LEVELS)):
        det, thr, _ = fin["xgb"]
        for cps in FRONTIER_CPS:
            w, p, _ = optimized_windows(det, cps, rng, net, hyper, f"frontier_{cps:g}", n=12, generations=8)
            sc = det.score(w)
            fr_rows.append({"round": rnd, "model": "xgb", "min_cps": cps, "achieved_cps": p["opt_cps"],
                            "windows": len(w), **{f"recall@{tg}": float(np.mean(sc > thr[tg])) for tg in TARGETS}})
    fr = pd.DataFrame(fr_rows)

    # ---------------- outputs
    for name, s in (("round0", s0), ("round1", s1)):
        s.insert(0, "round", name)
        s.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / f"adversarial_{name}_metrics.csv", index=False)
    fr.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "adversarial_frontier.csv", index=False)
    pd.concat([h_opt.assign(target="round0_xgb"), h_opt2.assign(target="round1_xgb")]).to_csv(
        C.TAB_DIR / "adversarial_optimizer_history.csv", index=False)
    json.dump({"optimized_vs_round0_xgb": p_opt, "optimized_r2_vs_round1_xgb": p_opt2},
              open(C.TAB_DIR / "adversarial_optimized_params.json", "w"), indent=2)
    plot_main({"Round 0: trained on humans + auto_fixed": s0,
               "Round 1: adversarial training (+ uniform, gaussian, human_like, optimized)": s1},
              {"Round 0: trained on humans + auto_fixed": LEVELS,
               "Round 1: adversarial training (+ uniform, gaussian, human_like, optimized)": levels1},
              C.FIG_DIR / "adversarial_recall_vs_level.png")
    plot_frontier(fr, C.FIG_DIR / "adversarial_utility_frontier.png")

    fpr = s0[["model", "fpr@0.01", "fpr@0.01_lo", "fpr@0.01_hi", "fpr@0.001"]].merge(
        s1[["model", "fpr@0.01", "fpr@0.01_lo", "fpr@0.01_hi", "fpr@0.001"]], on="model",
        suffixes=("_round0", "_round1"))
    fpr.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "adversarial_human_fpr.csv", index=False)
    rep = [f"# Phase E - adversarial experiment ({C.DEMO_TAG})", "",
           f"Optimized adversary: (mu, lambda) evolution strategy, minimum {MIN_CPS:g} CPS within bouts, "
           "objective = mean XGBoost score of its windows.", "",
           f"- vs round-0 XGBoost: median {p_opt['median_ms']:.1f} ms, spread {p_opt['spread']:.3f}, rho "
           f"{p_opt['rho']:.2f}, pause_prob {p_opt['pause_prob']:.3f}; achieved {p_opt['opt_cps']:.1f} CPS, "
           f"mean score {p_opt['opt_score']:.3f}",
           f"- vs round-1 XGBoost (optimized_r2): median {p_opt2['median_ms']:.1f} ms, spread "
           f"{p_opt2['spread']:.3f}, rho {p_opt2['rho']:.2f}, pause_prob {p_opt2['pause_prob']:.3f}; achieved "
           f"{p_opt2['opt_cps']:.1f} CPS, mean score {p_opt2['opt_score']:.3f}", "",
           "## Round 0 - recall at 1% FPR per level", "",
           md_table(s0, ["model"] + [f"recall@0.01[{l}]" for l in LEVELS]),
           "## Round 1 (adversarial training) - recall at 1% FPR per level", "",
           md_table(s1, ["model"] + [f"recall@0.01[{l}]" for l in levels1]),
           "## Human FPR before/after adversarial training (realised on test humans)", "",
           md_table(fpr, list(fpr.columns[:1]) + ["fpr@0.01_round0", "fpr@0.01_round1",
                                                  "fpr@0.001_round0", "fpr@0.001_round1"]),
           "## Utility frontier (XGBoost)", "", md_table(fr, list(fr.columns)), ""]
    (C.RESULTS_DIR / "phase_e_results.md").write_text("\n".join(rep), encoding="utf-8")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/adversary.py`:

```py
"""Phase B - synthetic click-automation adversaries (numeric interval sequences only).

This module only produces arrays of timestamps for offline evaluation. It contains no code that
interacts with a game client or injects input.

Levels (increasing sophistication):
  none       fixed CPS (classic autoclicker)
  uniform    fixed mean + uniform jitter
  gaussian   fixed mean + gaussian jitter
  human_like i.i.d. lognormal intervals with the SAME marginal hyper-distribution as the synthetic
             humans (a recorded-and-resampled human distribution without its temporal structure)
  optimized  parameters found by an evolution strategy that minimises the score of a chosen
             detector subject to a minimum click rate (the attacker wants an advantage)

All levels use the same bout structure (the clicker is switched on during fights) and the same
observation model (client-tick quantization + jitter) as the humans, so only the within-bout
timing differs.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as C
from .observation import NetParams
from .synth_humans import PLACEHOLDER_HYPER, _frames, to_recorder_frames

LEVELS = ["none", "uniform", "gaussian", "human_like", "optimized"]
LABEL_OF = {"none": "auto_fixed"}  # every other level is recorded as auto_jitter


@dataclass
class AttackParams:
    level: str
    median_ms: float            # centre of the interval distribution
    spread: float = 0.0         # uniform half-width (relative) / gaussian sd (relative) / lognormal sigma
    rho: float = 0.0            # AR(1) of log deviations (optimized only)
    pause_prob: float = 0.0     # probability of a short pause after each click (optimized only)
    pause_median_ms: float = 400.0
    bout_s: tuple = field(default=(3.0, 15.0))
    gap_s: tuple = field(default=(0.5, 4.0))


def sample_attack(level: str, rng: np.random.Generator, hyper: dict = PLACEHOLDER_HYPER,
                  opt: dict | None = None) -> AttackParams:
    cps = rng.uniform(8, 20)
    if level == "none":
        return AttackParams(level, 1000 / cps)
    if level == "uniform":
        return AttackParams(level, 1000 / cps, spread=rng.uniform(0.10, 0.50))
    if level == "gaussian":
        return AttackParams(level, 1000 / cps, spread=rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
    if level == "human_like":
        return AttackParams(level, rng.uniform(*hyper["median_ms"]), spread=rng.uniform(*hyper["sigma"]))
    if level == "optimized":
        if opt is None:
            raise ValueError("optimized level needs the parameters found by optimize_attack")
        p = {k: float(opt[k]) for k in OPT_BOUNDS}
        # small per-attacker perturbation around the optimum
        p["median_ms"] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.03)))
        p["spread"] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.05)))
        return AttackParams(level="optimized", **p)
    raise ValueError(level)


def _next_interval(p: AttackParams, rng: np.random.Generator, dev: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    if p.level == "none":
        return p.median_ms, 0.0
    if p.level == "uniform":
        return p.median_ms * (1 + rng.uniform(-p.spread, p.spread)), 0.0
    if p.level == "gaussian":
        return p.median_ms * (1 + rng.normal(0, p.spread)), 0.0
    # lognormal family (human_like: rho = 0; optimized: free rho)
    dev = p.rho * dev + np.sqrt(max(1 - p.rho ** 2, 0)) * rng.normal(0, p.spread)
    iv = p.median_ms * np.exp(dev)
    if p.pause_prob > 0 and rng.random() < p.pause_prob:
        iv += float(rng.lognormal(np.log(p.pause_median_ms), 0.5))
    return iv, dev


def attack_click_times(p: AttackParams, duration_s: float, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
    T = duration_s * 1000.0
    t, out = rng.uniform(0, 1000), []
    while t < T:
        bout_end = t + rng.uniform(*p.bout_s) * 1000
        dev = 0.0
        while t < min(bout_end, T):
            out.append(t)
            iv, dev = _next_interval(p, rng, dev)
            t += max(1.0, iv)
        t += rng.uniform(*p.gap_s) * 1000
    return np.asarray(out)


def generate_attackers(level: str, n_participants: int, rng: np.random.Generator, net: NetParams,
                       hyper: dict = PLACEHOLDER_HYPER, opt: dict | None = None,
                       sessions: int = 3, duration_s: float = 180.0, prefix: str = "SA"):
    cl, br, ss, pr = [], [], [], []
    label = LABEL_OF.get(level, "auto_jitter")
    for i in range(n_participants):
        pid = f"{prefix}-{level}-{i:03d}"
        p = sample_attack(level, rng, hyper, opt)
        pr.append({"participant": pid, **asdict(p)})
        for j in range(sessions):
            sid = f"{pid}-s{j}"
            c, b = to_recorder_frames(sid, label, attack_click_times(p, duration_s, rng), rng, net)
            cl.append(c); br.append(b)
            ss.append({"session_id": sid, "participant": pid, "label": label,
                       "duration_s": int(duration_s), "events_dropped": 0, "level": level})
    return _frames(cl, br, ss), pd.DataFrame(pr)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ optimized adversary
OPT_BOUNDS = {
    "median_ms": (40.0, 250.0),
    "spread": (0.0, 0.8),
    "rho": (-0.5, 0.9),
    "pause_prob": (0.0, 0.3),
    "pause_median_ms": (100.0, 800.0),
}


def effective_cps(p: dict, rng: np.random.Generator, n: int = 4000) -> float:
    """Mean click rate within bouts (Monte Carlo, before quantization)."""
    ap = AttackParams(level="optimized", **p)
    dev, tot = 0.0, 0.0
    for _ in range(n):
        iv, dev = _next_interval(ap, rng, dev)
        tot += max(1.0, iv)
    return 1000.0 * n / tot


def optimize_attack(score_fn, min_cps: float, rng: np.random.Generator, generations: int = 12,
                    mu: int = 4, lam: int = 12, eval_sessions: int = 4, duration_s: float = 60.0,
                    net: NetParams | None = None) -> tuple[dict, pd.DataFrame]:
    """(mu, lambda) evolution strategy in a normalised parameter space.

    score_fn(clicks, breaks, sessions) -> mean detector score (lower = less suspicious).
    Candidates violating the click-rate constraint are penalised. Common random numbers
    (same seeds per generation) reduce noise between candidates.
    """
    net = net or NetParams()
    keys = list(OPT_BOUNDS)
    lo = np.array([OPT_BOUNDS[k][0] for k in keys]); hi = np.array([OPT_BOUNDS[k][1] for k in keys])
    def decode(z):
        return {k: float(v) for k, v in zip(keys, lo + np.clip(z, 0, 1) * (hi - lo))}
    def evaluate(z, seed):
        p = decode(z)
        r = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        cps = effective_cps(p, r, 2000)
        cl, br, ss = [], [], []
        for j in range(eval_sessions):
            ap = AttackParams(level="optimized", **p)
            c, b = to_recorder_frames(f"opt-{j}", "auto_jitter", attack_click_times(ap, duration_s, r), r, net)
            cl.append(c); br.append(b)
            ss.append({"session_id": f"opt-{j}", "participant": "opt", "label": "auto_jitter",
                       "duration_s": int(duration_s), "events_dropped": 0, "level": "optimized"})
        s = score_fn(*_frames(cl, br, ss))
        penalty = max(0.0, min_cps - cps) * 0.5
        return s + penalty, s, cps
    pop = rng.uniform(0, 1, size=(lam, len(keys)))
    step = 0.25
    hist = []
    best = (np.inf, None, None, None)
    for g in range(generations):
        seed = int(rng.integers(1 << 31))
        res = [evaluate(z, seed) for z in pop]
        fit = np.array([r[0] for r in res])
        order = np.argsort(fit)
        for rank, i in enumerate(order[:mu]):
            hist.append({"generation": g, "rank": rank, "fitness": fit[i], "score": res[i][1],
                         "cps": res[i][2], **decode(pop[i])})
        if fit[order[0]] < best[0]:
            best = (fit[order[0]], pop[order[0]].copy(), res[order[0]][1], res[order[0]][2])
        parents = pop[order[:mu]]
        pop = np.vstack([parents[rng.integers(mu)] + rng.normal(0, step, len(keys)) for _ in range(lam - 1)]
                        + [best[1]])
        pop = np.clip(pop, 0, 1)
        step *= 0.85
    p = decode(best[1])
    p.update({"opt_score": float(best[2]), "opt_cps": float(best[3]), "min_cps": float(min_cps)})
    return p, pd.DataFrame(hist)

```

`ml/src/config.py`:

```py
"""Shared constants, paths and seeds for the ML pipeline.

Every constant that also exists in the Java plugin is marked with (JAVA) so the two
implementations can be kept in sync (see plugin ClickFeatureExtractor / ClickStreamCleaner).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths
ML_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPO_DIR = ML_DIR.parent
# Real data location. Overridable with ML_RAW_DIR (run_all.ps1 -RawDir) so the pipeline can
# read data stored outside the repository (the raw folder is git-ignored).
RAW_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("ML_RAW_DIR", ML_DIR / "data" / "raw"))
PROCESSED_DIR = ML_DIR / "data" / "processed"
RESULTS_DIR = ML_DIR / "results"
FIG_DIR = RESULTS_DIR / "figures"
TAB_DIR = RESULTS_DIR / "tables"
MODEL_DIR = RESULTS_DIR / "models"
PLUGIN_RES_DIR = REPO_DIR / "plugin" / "src" / "main" / "resources"

for _d in (PROCESSED_DIR, FIG_DIR, TAB_DIR, MODEL_DIR):
    _d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- reproducibility
SEED = 20261011

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- cleaning (JAVA)
TICK_MS = 50.0              # one game tick at 20 TPS (client and server)
DEDUP_MS = 15.0             # swings closer than this to the last accepted swing are duplicates
HELD_MAX_IV_MS = 65.0       # swing-to-swing interval compatible with "button held" (1 tick + jitter)
FRESH_DIG_MS = 15.0         # a swing within this time of an interact_block belongs to that dig start
PAUSE_MS = 1000.0           # intervals above this are pauses, excluded from distribution stats
TRIM_MOVES_S = 5.0          # spawn / gravity-drop trim at the start of each session (moves)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- windows (JAVA)
WINDOW_CLICKS = 50
STRIDE_CLICKS = 25
WINDOW_S = 5.0
STRIDE_S = 2.5
MIN_INTERVALS_PER_WINDOW = 10   # time windows with fewer usable intervals are discarded
HELD_ROUTE_FRAC = 0.5           # windows with >= this fraction of held swings are routed to "mining"
TICK_NEAR_MS = 5.0              # |interval - k*50ms| <= this counts as "near a tick multiple"
HIST_EDGES_MS = [float(x) for x in range(0, 525, 25)]  # 20 bins of 25 ms on [0, 500) + overflow

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ORIGINAL thesis thresholds
# Annex I: flag if diff > 0 && diff < (1000 / maxClicksPerSecond) with integer division -> 66 ms
ORIG_MAX_CPS = 15
ORIG_CLICK_MIN_DIFF_MS = 1000 // ORIG_MAX_CPS        # = 66 (Java integer division)
ORIG_FASTBREAK_MIN_MS = 450                          # config.yml min_delay_ms
ORIG_SPEED_TEXT = 1.2                                # blocks/tick, thesis text (4.6.1)
# config.yml ships 0.1 but loadConfigValues clamps to [0.5, 5.0] -> effective 0.5
ORIG_SPEED_EFFECTIVE = 0.5
ORIG_FLY_DY = 1.0
ORIG_MAX_VIOLATIONS = 15

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- labels
LEGACY_LABELS = {"human": "human_unspecified"}
HUMAN_LABELS = {"human_combat", "human_mining", "human_explore", "human_unspecified"}
CLICK_AUTO_LABELS = {"auto_fixed", "auto_jitter"}
MOVE_CHEAT_LABELS = {"speed_mod", "fly_mod"}
ALL_LABELS = HUMAN_LABELS | CLICK_AUTO_LABELS | MOVE_CHEAT_LABELS

ACTIVITY = {
    "human_combat": "combat", "human_mining": "mining", "human_explore": "explore",
    "human_unspecified": "unspecified", "auto_fixed": "auto", "auto_jitter": "auto",
    "speed_mod": "movement_cheat", "fly_mod": "movement_cheat",
}

# Banner used on every figure/table that involves synthetic humans
DEMO_TAG = "DEMO - synthetic, not evidence about real players"

`ml/src/data.py`:

```py
"""Phase A - data layer.

Loads the CSV sessions written by SessionRecorder, validates their schema, normalises legacy
labels, applies the cleaning rules derived from the first real recordings and computes the
per-session click streams/intervals used by every later phase.

Run as a script to (re)write results/data_report.md and data/processed/*.csv:
    python -m src.data
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import stats

from . import config as C

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

SCHEMAS = {
    "clicks": ["session_id", "t_ns", "source", "label"],
    "moves": ["session_id", "t_ns", "dx", "dy", "dz", "on_ground", "tps", "ping", "label"],
    "breaks": ["session_id", "t_ns", "label"],
    "sessions": ["session_id", "participant", "label", "start_utc", "end_utc",
                 "duration_s", "events_dropped"],
}
CLICK_SOURCES = {"swing", "interact_air", "interact_block"}


class SchemaError(ValueError):
    pass


@dataclass
class Dataset:
    clicks: pd.DataFrame
    moves: pd.DataFrame
    breaks: pd.DataFrame
    sessions: pd.DataFrame
    issues: list[str]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ loading / validation
def normalise_label(label: str) -> str:
    label = str(label).strip()
    return C.LEGACY_LABELS.get(label, label)


def _read(path: Path, kind: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = pd.read_csv(path, sep=",", dtype=str, keep_default_na=False)
    if list(df.columns) != SCHEMAS[kind]:
        raise SchemaError(f"{path}: expected columns {SCHEMAS[kind]}, got {list(df.columns)}")
    return df


def _coerce(df: pd.DataFrame, kind: str, path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = df.copy()
    num = {
        "clicks": {"t_ns": "int64"},
        "moves": {"t_ns": "int64", "dx": "float", "dy": "float", "dz": "float",
                  "on_ground": "int64", "tps": "float", "ping": "int64"},
        "breaks": {"t_ns": "int64"},
        "sessions": {"duration_s": "int64", "events_dropped": "int64"},
    }[kind]
    for col, dtype in num.items():
        try:
            df[col] = pd.to_numeric(df[col], errors="raise").astype(dtype)
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as e:
            raise SchemaError(f"{path}: column {col} is not numeric ({e})") from e
    if "label" in df:
        df["label"] = df["label"].map(normalise_label)
        bad = set(df["label"]) - C.ALL_LABELS
        if bad:
            raise SchemaError(f"{path}: unknown labels {sorted(bad)}")
    if kind == "clicks":
        bad = set(df["source"]) - CLICK_SOURCES
        if bad:
            raise SchemaError(f"{path}: unknown click sources {sorted(bad)}")
    if "t_ns" in df and (df["t_ns"] < 0).any():
        raise SchemaError(f"{path}: negative t_ns")
    return df


def load_raw(raw_dir: Path | None = None, origin: str = "real") -> Dataset:
    """Load every <participant>/ folder under raw_dir. Missing folders -> empty dataset."""
    raw_dir = Path(raw_dir or C.RAW_DIR)
    parts = {k: [] for k in SCHEMAS}
    issues: list[str] = []
    if not raw_dir.exists():
        issues.append(f"raw data directory {raw_dir} does not exist")
    for pdir in sorted(p for p in raw_dir.glob("*") if p.is_dir()) if raw_dir.exists() else []:
        for kind in SCHEMAS:
            f = pdir / f"{kind}.csv"
            if not f.exists():
                issues.append(f"{pdir.name}: missing {kind}.csv")
                continue
            df = _coerce(_read(f, kind), kind, f)
            df["participant_dir"] = pdir.name
            parts[kind].append(df)
    out = {k: (pd.concat(v, ignore_index=True) if v else
               pd.DataFrame(columns=SCHEMAS[k] + ["participant_dir"])) for k, v in parts.items()}
    sessions = out["sessions"].drop_duplicates("session_id")
    # sessions.csv row is written at /record stop. Sessions without it (crash) are kept but flagged.
    seen = set(sessions["session_id"])
    for kind in ("clicks", "moves", "breaks"):
        orphan = sorted(set(out[kind]["session_id"]) - seen)
        for sid in orphan:
            sub = out[kind][out[kind]["session_id"] == sid]
            issues.append(f"session {sid}: present in {kind}.csv but not in sessions.csv (kept, incomplete)")
            sessions = pd.concat([sessions, pd.DataFrame([{
                "session_id": sid, "participant": sub["participant_dir"].iloc[0],
                "label": sub["label"].iloc[0], "start_utc": "", "end_utc": "",
                "duration_s": int(sub["t_ns"].max() // 1e9), "events_dropped": -1,
                "participant_dir": sub["participant_dir"].iloc[0]}])], ignore_index=True)
            seen.add(sid)
    for _, s in sessions.iterrows():
        if s["participant"] != s["participant_dir"]:
            issues.append(f"session {s['session_id']}: participant {s['participant']} stored in folder {s['participant_dir']}")
        if s["events_dropped"] > 0:
            issues.append(f"session {s['session_id']}: {s['events_dropped']} events dropped by recorder backpressure")
    # event label must match the session label
    lab = sessions.set_index("session_id")["label"]
    for kind in ("clicks", "moves", "breaks"):
        df = out[kind]
        if len(df):
            mism = df[df["label"] != df["session_id"].map(lab)]
            if len(mism):
                issues.append(f"{kind}: {len(mism)} rows whose label differs from sessions.csv")
    sessions = sessions.copy()
    sessions["activity"] = sessions["label"].map(C.ACTIVITY)
    sessions["origin"] = origin
    # real sessions: the "level" is the recorded label (auto_fixed / auto_jitter / human_*)
    sessions["level"] = sessions["label"].map(lambda l: "human" if l in C.HUMAN_LABELS else f"real_{l}")
    for kind in ("clicks", "moves", "breaks"):
        out[kind] = out[kind].merge(sessions[["session_id", "participant"]], on="session_id", how="left")
        out[kind]["origin"] = origin
    return Dataset(out["clicks"], out["moves"], out["breaks"], sessions.reset_index(drop=True), issues)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ click stream cleaning
def clean_click_stream(clicks: pd.DataFrame, breaks: pd.DataFrame | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Causal cleaning of ONE session (same algorithm as the Java ClickStreamCleaner).

    1. Only `swing` events are clicks; interact_* are used solely as dig-start markers.
    2. A swing closer than DEDUP_MS to the last *accepted* swing is a duplicate (counted, dropped).
    3. Held-button (mining) state machine. A dig marker (interact_block or break) opens a dig;
       while the button stays held the client sends one swing per tick, so the dig run lasts
       while consecutive swings are <= HELD_MAX_IV_MS apart (a break does NOT end it: in the real
       data the break arrives before the held swings when blocks break instantly):
         interact_block | break -> digging = True, last_dig = t
         swing          -> fresh = t - last_dig <= FRESH_DIG_MS
                           held  = digging and iv <= HELD_MAX_IV_MS and (not fresh or prev_held)
                           if not held and not fresh: digging = False
    Returns one row per accepted swing: t_ms, iv_ms (NaN for the first), held, n_dup.
    """
    ev = [(float(t) / 1e6, src) for t, src in zip(clicks["t_ns"], clicks["source"])]
    if breaks is not None and len(breaks):
        ev += [(float(t) / 1e6, "break") for t in breaks["t_ns"]]
    # tie order: dig start, then break, then swing (ties are practically impossible with ns)
    prio = {"interact_block": 0, "interact_air": 1, "break": 2, "swing": 3}
    ev.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], prio[x[1]]))

    rows: list[list] = []
    digging, prev_held = False, False
    last_dig = -math.inf
    last_acc = None
    for t, src in ev:
        if src in ("interact_block", "break"):
            digging, last_dig = True, t
        elif src == "swing":
            if last_acc is not None and t - last_acc < C.DEDUP_MS:
                rows[-1][3] += 1
                continue
            iv = math.nan if last_acc is None else t - last_acc
            fresh = (t - last_dig) <= C.FRESH_DIG_MS
            held = bool(digging and not math.isnan(iv) and iv <= C.HELD_MAX_IV_MS
                        and (not fresh or prev_held))
            if not held and not fresh:
                digging = False
            prev_held = held
            rows.append([t, iv, held, 0])
            last_acc = t
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["t_ms", "iv_ms", "held", "n_dup"])


def build_click_streams(ds: Dataset) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Clean every session; returns accepted swings with session metadata."""
    out = []
    br = ds.breaks.groupby("session_id") if len(ds.breaks) else None
    for sid, cl in ds.clicks.groupby("session_id"):
        b = br.get_group(sid) if br is not None and sid in br.groups else None
        s = clean_click_stream(cl, b)
        s.insert(0, "session_id", sid)
        out.append(s)
    cols = ["session_id", "t_ms", "iv_ms", "held", "n_dup"]
    df = pd.concat(out, ignore_index=True) if out else pd.DataFrame(columns=cols)
    meta = ds.sessions[[c for c in ["session_id", "participant", "label", "activity", "origin", "level"]
                        if c in ds.sessions.columns]]
    return df.merge(meta, on="session_id", how="left")


def naive_intervals(clicks: pd.DataFrame, sources: set[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Intervals between consecutive raw events of the given sources, NO de-duplication.

    sources={'interact_air','interact_block'} reproduces what the Java onPlayerInteract
    detector sees; using all sources shows the effect of one physical click -> 3 events.
    Java uses System.currentTimeMillis(), so differences are floored to whole ms.
    """
    out = []
    for sid, g in clicks[clicks["source"].isin(sources)].groupby("session_id"):
        t = np.sort(g["t_ns"].to_numpy()) / 1e6
        if len(t) < 2:
            continue
        out.append(pd.DataFrame({"session_id": sid, "iv_ms": np.floor(np.diff(t))}))
    return pd.concat(out, ignore_index=True) if out else pd.DataFrame(columns=["session_id", "iv_ms"])


def trim_moves(moves: pd.DataFrame, seconds: float = C.TRIM_MOVES_S) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Drop the first `seconds` after the FIRST move event of every session (spawn gravity drop).

    Relative to the first move, not to session start: in the real data moves begin ~12 s after
    /record start.
    """
    if moves.empty:
        return moves
    first = moves.groupby("session_id")["t_ns"].transform("min")
    return moves[moves["t_ns"] - first >= seconds * 1e9].reset_index(drop=True)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ tick quantization
def tick_residuals(iv_ms: np.ndarray, tick: float = C.TICK_MS) -> np.ndarray:
    """Signed distance of each interval to the nearest multiple of one tick, in (-25, 25]."""
    iv = np.asarray(iv_ms, dtype=float)
    return iv - tick * np.round(iv / tick)


def quantization_summary(iv_ms: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    iv = np.asarray(iv_ms, dtype=float)
    iv = iv[(iv > 0) & (iv < C.PAUSE_MS)]
    if len(iv) == 0:
        return {"n": 0}
    r = tick_residuals(iv)
    # Under "no quantization" residuals would be ~uniform on (-25, 25]
    ks = stats.kstest(r, stats.uniform(loc=-25, scale=50).cdf)
    return {
        "n": int(len(iv)),
        "frac_within_5ms": float(np.mean(np.abs(r) <= C.TICK_NEAR_MS)),
        "frac_within_5ms_if_uniform": C.TICK_NEAR_MS * 2 / C.TICK_MS,
        "residual_mean_ms": float(np.mean(r)),
        "residual_sd_ms": float(np.std(r, ddof=1)) if len(r) > 1 else float("nan"),
        "residual_mad_ms": float(stats.median_abs_deviation(r)),
        "ks_vs_uniform_stat": float(ks.statistic),
        "ks_vs_uniform_p": float(ks.pvalue),
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ baselines on raw data
def baseline_event_flags(ds: Dataset, streams: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Per-event application of the ORIGINAL thesis thresholds (Annex I) on real data."""
    rows = []
    thr = C.ORIG_CLICK_MIN_DIFF_MS
    def cnt(iv):  # Java: diff > 0 && diff < 66
        iv = np.asarray(iv, dtype=float)
        iv = iv[~np.isnan(iv)]
        return int(len(iv)), int(np.sum((iv > 0) & (iv < thr)))
    for sid, sess in ds.sessions.set_index("session_id").iterrows():
        cl = ds.clicks[ds.clicks["session_id"] == sid]
        n1, f1 = cnt(naive_intervals(cl, {"interact_air", "interact_block"})["iv_ms"])
        n2, f2 = cnt(naive_intervals(cl, CLICK_SOURCES)["iv_ms"])
        st = streams[streams["session_id"] == sid]
        n3, f3 = cnt(np.floor(st["iv_ms"]))
        act = st[~st["held"].astype(bool)]
        n4, f4 = cnt(np.floor(act["iv_ms"]))
        bt = np.sort(ds.breaks.loc[ds.breaks["session_id"] == sid, "t_ns"].to_numpy()) / 1e6
        bd = np.floor(np.diff(bt)) if len(bt) > 1 else np.array([])
        mv = trim_moves(ds.moves[ds.moves["session_id"] == sid])
        mv_all = ds.moves[ds.moves["session_id"] == sid]
        def mvflags(m):
            h = np.hypot(m["dx"], m["dz"])
            fly = (m["on_ground"] == 0) & (m["dy"].abs() > C.ORIG_FLY_DY)
            return (int(len(m)), int((h > C.ORIG_SPEED_TEXT).sum()),
                    int((h > C.ORIG_SPEED_EFFECTIVE).sum()), int(fly.sum()))
        a = mvflags(mv_all); b = mvflags(mv)
        rows.append({
            "session_id": sid, "participant": sess["participant"], "label": sess["label"],
            "naive_java_interact_n": n1, "naive_java_interact_flags": f1,
            "naive_all_events_n": n2, "naive_all_events_flags": f2,
            "dedup_swing_n": n3, "dedup_swing_flags": f3,
            "cleaned_active_n": n4, "cleaned_active_flags": f4,
            "breaks_intervals_n": int(len(bd)),
            "fastbreak_flags": int(np.sum(bd < C.ORIG_FASTBREAK_MIN_MS)),
            "moves_untrimmed_n": a[0], "speed1.2_flags_untrimmed": a[1],
            "speed0.5_flags_untrimmed": a[2], "fly_flags_untrimmed": a[3],
            "moves_trimmed_n": b[0], "speed1.2_flags": b[1], "speed0.5_flags": b[2],
            "fly_flags": b[3],
        })
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ report
def _md_table(df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    if df.empty:
        return "_(empty)_\n"
    cols = list(df.columns)
    lines = ["| " + " | ".join(map(str, cols)) + " |", "|" + "---|" * len(cols)]
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        lines.append("| " + " | ".join(
            f"{v:.3g}" if isinstance(v, float) else str(v) for v in r.tolist()) + " |")
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def write_report(ds: Dataset, streams: pd.DataFrame, flags: pd.DataFrame, quant: dict,
                 path: Path = C.RESULTS_DIR / "data_report.md") -> None:
    s = ds.sessions
    lines = ["# Data report (REAL data, generated by `src/data.py`)", "",
             f"Raw directory: `{C.RAW_DIR.name}/` (git-ignored). Regenerate with `run_all.ps1`.", ""]
    if s.empty:
        lines += ["**No real sessions found.** Every later result is synthetic (DEMO).", ""]
    else:
        ev = pd.DataFrame({
            "clicks": ds.clicks.groupby("session_id").size(),
            "swings": ds.clicks[ds.clicks.source == "swing"].groupby("session_id").size(),
            "accepted_swings": streams.groupby("session_id").size(),
            "held_swings": streams.groupby("session_id")["held"].sum(),
            "duplicates_merged": streams.groupby("session_id")["n_dup"].sum(),
            "moves": ds.moves.groupby("session_id").size(),
            "breaks": ds.breaks.groupby("session_id").size(),
        }).fillna(0).astype(int)
        per = s.set_index("session_id")[["participant", "label", "duration_s", "events_dropped"]].join(ev).reset_index()
        lines += ["## Sessions", "", _md_table(per)]
        by_label = s.groupby("label").agg(sessions=("session_id", "size"),
                                          participants=("participant", "nunique"),
                                          duration_s=("duration_s", "sum"),
                                          events_dropped=("events_dropped", "sum")).reset_index()
        by_part = s.groupby("participant").agg(sessions=("session_id", "size"),
                                               labels=("label", lambda x: ",".join(sorted(set(x)))),
                                               duration_s=("duration_s", "sum"),
                                               events_dropped=("events_dropped", "sum")).reset_index()
        src = ds.clicks.groupby("source").size().rename("events").reset_index()
        lines += ["## Counts per label", "", _md_table(by_label),
                  "## Counts per participant", "", _md_table(by_part),
                  "## Click events per source (raw)", "", _md_table(src)]
        raw_gaps = []
        for _, g in ds.clicks.groupby("session_id"):
            t = np.sort(g["t_ns"].to_numpy()) / 1e6
            raw_gaps.append(np.diff(t))
        raw_gaps = np.concatenate(raw_gaps) if raw_gaps else np.array([])
        lines += [f"Raw click events closer than {C.DEDUP_MS:.0f} ms to the previous event (any source): "
                  f"**{int(np.sum(raw_gaps < C.DEDUP_MS))}** of {len(raw_gaps)} gaps "
                  "(one physical click -> interact_block/interact_air/swing).", ""]
        # breaks arriving within 5 ms of a dig start = block broken instantly (creative mode or
        # instant-mine blocks); recorder does not log the game mode, so this is only an indicator
        inst = []
        for sid, g in ds.breaks.groupby("session_id"):
            dig = np.sort(ds.clicks.loc[(ds.clicks.session_id == sid) &
                                        (ds.clicks.source == "interact_block"), "t_ns"].to_numpy()) / 1e6
            for bt in g["t_ns"].to_numpy() / 1e6:
                k = np.searchsorted(dig, bt, "right") - 1
                inst.append(k >= 0 and bt - dig[k] <= 5.0)
        if inst:
            lines += [f"Breaks arriving <= 5 ms after an interact_block (instant breaks): "
                      f"**{int(np.sum(inst))} of {len(inst)}**. Many instant breaks suggest creative mode or "
                      "instant-mine blocks; the recorder does not store the game mode.", ""]
        lines += ["## Issues found during validation", ""]
        lines += [f"- {i}" for i in ds.issues] or ["- none"]
        lines += ["", "## Timing quantization (accepted swings, intervals < 1 s)", "",
                  "`all_*` = held + active swings (held swings are one per client tick by construction);",
                  "`active_*` = non-held swings only, i.e. real clicks. Only the active numbers speak",
                  "about click timing; with few active intervals they are anecdotal.", ""]
        lines += [f"- {k}: {v:.4g}" if isinstance(v, float) else f"- {k}: {v}" for k, v in quant.items()]
        lines += ["", "Interpretation: if the server saw continuous time, the distance of each interval to the",
                  "nearest multiple of 50 ms would be ~uniform on (-25, 25] ms and only "
                  f"{quant.get('all_frac_within_5ms_if_uniform', 0):.0%} of intervals would fall within +-5 ms.",
                  "See `figures/real_tick_quantization.png`.", "",
                  "## Original thesis thresholds applied per event (REAL data)", "",
                  "Columns: `naive_java_interact` = consecutive LEFT_CLICK_AIR/BLOCK events exactly as",
                  "`onPlayerInteract` sees them; `naive_all_events` = every click event without de-duplication;",
                  "`dedup_swing` = swings after 15 ms de-duplication; `cleaned_active` = de-duplicated swings",
                  "excluding held-button (mining) swings. Click flag: 0 < diff < 66 ms (1000/15, integer",
                  "division as in Annex I). FastBreak: break interval < 450 ms. Speed: horizontal",
                  "displacement per move event > 1.2 (thesis text) or > 0.5 (effective value: config.yml",
                  "ships 0.1, clamped to 0.5 by loadConfigValues). Fly: not on ground and |dy| > 1.0.",
                  "Moves are reported with and without the 5 s spawn trim.", "",
                  _md_table(flags.T.reset_index().rename(columns={"index": "metric"}) if len(flags) else flags),
                  "All sessions are labelled human: every flag above is a false positive of the",
                  "corresponding rule on legitimate play (tiny sample: anecdotal, not a rate estimate).", ""]
    path.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")


def plot_quantization(iv_ms: np.ndarray, path: Path) -> None:
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    iv = np.asarray(iv_ms, dtype=float)
    iv = iv[(iv > 0) & (iv < C.PAUSE_MS)]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(10, 3.6))
    ax[0].hist(iv, bins=np.arange(0, 1000, 5), color="#3b6ea5")
    for k in range(1, 20):
        ax[0].axvline(k * 50, color="grey", lw=0.5, ls=":")
    ax[0].set(xlabel="interval between accepted swings (ms)", ylabel="count",
              title=f"REAL swing intervals (n={len(iv)})")
    ax[1].hist(tick_residuals(iv), bins=np.arange(-25, 26, 2.5), color="#3b6ea5")
    ax[1].set(xlabel="distance to nearest multiple of 50 ms (ms)", ylabel="count",
              title="REAL: residual to tick grid")
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=130)
    plt.close(fig)


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    ds = load_raw()
    streams = build_click_streams(ds)
    flags = baseline_event_flags(ds, streams) if len(ds.sessions) else pd.DataFrame()
    iv = streams["iv_ms"].dropna().to_numpy() if len(streams) else np.array([])
    quant = {f"all_{k}": v for k, v in quantization_summary(iv).items()}
    act = streams.loc[~streams["held"].astype(bool), "iv_ms"].dropna().to_numpy() if len(streams) else iv
    quant.update({f"active_{k}": v for k, v in quantization_summary(act).items()})
    if len(iv):
        plot_quantization(iv, C.FIG_DIR / "real_tick_quantization.png")
    write_report(ds, streams, flags, quant)
    # Derived real data stays local (data/processed is git-ignored)
    streams.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "real_click_streams.csv", index=False)
    ds.sessions.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "real_sessions.csv", index=False)
    pd.DataFrame([quant]).to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "real_quantization.csv", index=False)
    if len(flags):
        flags.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "real_baseline_event_flags.csv", index=False)
    log.info("sessions=%d accepted swings=%d issues=%d", len(ds.sessions), len(streams), len(ds.issues))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/evaluate.py`:

```py
"""Phase D - evaluation protocol.

Splits:   StratifiedGroupKFold over PARTICIPANTS (no participant in train and test).
          Inside each training fold, 25% of the HUMAN participants are held out as a calibration
          set used only to place the decision threshold at the target FPR. The test fold is never
          used for fitting or for thresholds.
Metrics:  recall at FPR <= 0.1% and <= 1% (threshold from calibration humans; the realised test
          FPR is reported next to it), PR-AUC, F1 and confusion matrix at the 1% threshold,
          session-level alarm rates, and 95% bootstrap CIs resampling participants within class.
Real data: if real windows of both classes from >= 2 groups exist the same protocol runs on them
          (grouped by participant, or by session with a flagged limitation). Otherwise the final
          synthetic-trained detectors are applied to the real human windows (FPR only).

    python -m src.evaluate
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import time

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score, precision_recall_curve
from sklearn.model_selection import GroupShuffleSplit, StratifiedGroupKFold

from . import config as C
from .data import build_click_streams, load_raw
from .features import make_windows, scored
from .train import FIXED_RULES, available_models, is_attack, make_detector

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
TARGETS = (0.001, 0.01)
STANDARD_ATTACKS = ["none", "uniform", "gaussian"]
N_BOOT = 1000


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ window preparation
def _violation_counts(t_start, t_end, ev_t, ev_flag):
    """Number of flagged raw gaps whose end lies within [t_start, t_end] (prefix sums)."""
    cs = np.r_[0, np.cumsum(ev_flag)]
    i0 = np.searchsorted(ev_t, t_start, "left")
    i1 = np.searchsorted(ev_t, t_end, "right")
    return cs[i1] - cs[i0]


def prepare_windows(streams: pd.DataFrame, raw_clicks: pd.DataFrame, window_type: str = "count",
                    moves: pd.DataFrame | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Scored windows + the two ORIGINAL-rule violation counts used by the fixed baselines."""
    w = scored(make_windows(streams, window_type, moves=moves, keep_sequences=True))
    if w.empty:
        return w
    thr = C.ORIG_CLICK_MIN_DIFF_MS
    w["clean_violations"] = [int(np.sum((np.floor(s) > 0) & (np.floor(s) < thr))) for s in w["seq"]]
    naive = np.zeros(len(w), dtype=int)
    inter = raw_clicks[raw_clicks["source"].isin(["interact_air", "interact_block"])]
    groups = {sid: np.sort(g["t_ns"].to_numpy()) / 1e6 for sid, g in inter.groupby("session_id")}
    for sid, idx in w.groupby("session_id").groups.items():
        t = groups.get(sid)
        if t is None or len(t) < 2:
            continue
        d = np.floor(np.diff(t))
        flag = ((d > 0) & (d < thr)).astype(int)
        sub = w.loc[idx]
        naive[np.asarray(idx)] = _violation_counts(sub["t_start_ms"].to_numpy(), sub["t_end_ms"].to_numpy(),
                                                   t[1:], flag)
    w["naive_java_violations"] = naive
    return w


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ thresholds
def calibrate(cal_scores: np.ndarray, target: float) -> float:
    """Smallest threshold whose FPR on the calibration humans is <= target (flag: score > thr)."""
    s = np.sort(np.asarray(cal_scores, dtype=float))
    k = int(np.floor(target * len(s)))          # number of calibration humans allowed above thr
    return float(s[len(s) - 1 - k]) if len(s) else float("inf")


def run_cv(win: pd.DataFrame, models: list[str], train_levels: list[str], eval_levels: list[str],
           group_col: str = "participant", n_splits: int = 5, seed: int = C.SEED,
           extra_train: pd.DataFrame | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Out-of-fold scores and per-fold thresholds for every model.

    win must contain humans (level == 'human') and attackers of every level in train/eval levels.
    """
    win = win.reset_index(drop=True)
    y_all = is_attack(win)
    groups = win[group_col].to_numpy()
    sgkf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=n_splits, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)
    out = []
    for fold, (tr, te) in enumerate(sgkf.split(win, y_all, groups)):
        trw, tew = win.iloc[tr], win.iloc[te]
        trw = trw[(trw["level"] == "human") | trw["level"].isin(train_levels)]
        tew = tew[(tew["level"] == "human") | tew["level"].isin(eval_levels)]
        hum = trw[trw["level"] == "human"]
        gss = GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=0.25, random_state=seed + fold)
        fit_i, cal_i = next(gss.split(hum, groups=hum[group_col]))
        cal = hum.iloc[cal_i]
        fit = pd.concat([hum.iloc[fit_i], trw[trw["level"] != "human"]] +
                        ([extra_train] if extra_train is not None else []))
        for m in models:
            t0 = time.time()
            det = make_detector(m, seed + fold).fit(fit)
            cs, ts = det.score(cal), det.score(tew)
            thr = {tg: (0.0 if m in FIXED_RULES else calibrate(cs, tg)) for tg in TARGETS}
            o = tew[["session_id", group_col, "label", "level", "origin"]].copy()
            if group_col != "participant":
                o["participant"] = tew["participant"]
            o["model"], o["fold"], o["score"], o["y"] = m, fold, ts, is_attack(tew)
            o["n_cal"] = len(cal)
            for tg in TARGETS:
                o[f"thr_{tg}"] = thr[tg]
                o[f"flag_{tg}"] = (ts > thr[tg]).astype(int)
            out.append(o)
            log.info("fold %d %-20s fit+score %.1fs (train=%d cal=%d test=%d)", fold, m,
                     time.time() - t0, len(fit), len(cal), len(tew))
    return pd.concat(out, ignore_index=True)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ metrics + bootstrap
def _rate_ci(per: pd.DataFrame, rng, n_boot=N_BOOT):
    """per: rows = participants with columns n, k. Bootstrap ratio sum(k)/sum(n)."""
    if per.empty or per["n"].sum() == 0:
        return float("nan"), float("nan"), float("nan")
    n, k = per["n"].to_numpy(float), per["k"].to_numpy(float)
    idx = rng.integers(0, len(per), size=(n_boot, len(per)))
    reps = k[idx].sum(1) / np.maximum(n[idx].sum(1), 1)
    return float(k.sum() / n.sum()), float(np.percentile(reps, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(reps, 97.5))


def summarize(oof: pd.DataFrame, rng=None, levels: list[str] | None = None,
              group_col: str = "participant") -> pd.DataFrame:
    rng = rng or np.random.default_rng(C.SEED)
    rows = []
    for m, g in oof.groupby("model", sort=False):
        hum = g[g["y"] == 0]
        r = {"model": m, "n_human_windows": len(hum), "n_attack_windows": int(g["y"].sum()),
             "n_human_groups": hum[group_col].nunique(), "n_cal_humans_mean": float(g["n_cal"].mean())}
        for tg in TARGETS:
            ph = hum.groupby(group_col)[f"flag_{tg}"].agg(n="size", k="sum")
            r[f"fpr@{tg}"], r[f"fpr@{tg}_lo"], r[f"fpr@{tg}_hi"] = _rate_ci(ph, rng)
            lv = levels or sorted(set(g.loc[g["y"] == 1, "level"]))
            for L in [None] + list(lv):
                a = g[(g["y"] == 1) & ((g["level"] == L) if L else True)]
                pa = a.groupby(group_col)[f"flag_{tg}"].agg(n="size", k="sum")
                key = f"recall@{tg}" + (f"[{L}]" if L else "")
                r[key], r[key + "_lo"], r[key + "_hi"] = _rate_ci(pa, rng)
        # threshold-free and 1%-threshold metrics
        if g["y"].nunique() == 2:
            r["pr_auc"] = float(average_precision_score(g["y"], g["score"]))
            parts = g[group_col].unique()
            reps = []
            for _ in range(200):
                pick = rng.choice(parts, len(parts), replace=True)
                gg = pd.concat([g[g[group_col] == p] for p in pick]) if len(parts) < 60 else \
                    g.set_index(group_col).loc[pick].reset_index()
                if gg["y"].nunique() == 2:
                    reps.append(average_precision_score(gg["y"], gg["score"]))
            r["pr_auc_lo"], r["pr_auc_hi"] = (float(np.percentile(reps, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(reps, 97.5))) if reps else (np.nan, np.nan)
        f = g["flag_0.01"]
        tp = int(((f == 1) & (g["y"] == 1)).sum()); fp = int(((f == 1) & (g["y"] == 0)).sum())
        fn = int(((f == 0) & (g["y"] == 1)).sum()); tn = int(((f == 0) & (g["y"] == 0)).sum())
        r.update({"tp@0.01": tp, "fp@0.01": fp, "fn@0.01": fn, "tn@0.01": tn,
                  "f1@0.01": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)})
        # session-level alarm: >= 3 flagged windows in a session (VL-like accumulation)
        s = g.groupby("session_id").agg(y=("y", "first"), k=("flag_0.01", "sum"))
        r["session_alarm_human"] = float((s.loc[s.y == 0, "k"] >= 3).mean()) if (s.y == 0).any() else np.nan
        r["session_alarm_attack"] = float((s.loc[s.y == 1, "k"] >= 3).mean()) if (s.y == 1).any() else np.nan
        rows.append(r)
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def md_table(df: pd.DataFrame, cols: list[str], fmt="{:.3f}") -> str:
    out = ["| " + " | ".join(cols) + " |", "|" + "---|" * len(cols)]
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        cells = []
        for c in cols:
            v = r.get(c)
            if isinstance(v, (float, np.floating)):
                lo, hi = r.get(c + "_lo"), r.get(c + "_hi")
                cells.append(fmt.format(v) + (f" [{fmt.format(lo)}, {fmt.format(hi)}]"
                                              if lo is not None and not pd.isna(lo) else ""))
            else:
                cells.append(str(v))
        out.append("| " + " | ".join(cells) + " |")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"


def plot_recall(summary: pd.DataFrame, path, title: str):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 3.6))
    x = np.arange(len(summary))
    for j, tg in enumerate(TARGETS):
        v = summary[f"recall@{tg}"].to_numpy()
        err = np.vstack([v - summary[f"recall@{tg}_lo"], summary[f"recall@{tg}_hi"] - v])
        ax.bar(x + (j - 0.5) * 0.38, v, 0.38, yerr=err, capsize=3,
               label=f"threshold at FPR {tg:.1%} (calibration humans)")
    ax.set_xticks(x, summary["model"], rotation=30, ha="right", fontsize=8)
    ax.set_ylabel("recall (attack windows)")
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
    ax.legend(fontsize=7)
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=130)
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_pr(oof: pd.DataFrame, path, title: str):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5, 4))
    for m, g in oof.groupby("model", sort=False):
        if m in FIXED_RULES or g["y"].nunique() < 2:
            continue
        p, r, _ = precision_recall_curve(g["y"], g["score"])
        ax.plot(r, p, label=f"{m} (AP={average_precision_score(g['y'], g['score']):.3f})", lw=1.2)
    ax.set(xlabel="recall", ylabel="precision", title=title)
    ax.title.set_fontsize(8)
    ax.legend(fontsize=7)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=130)
    plt.close(fig)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ data access
def synthetic_windows(window_type: str, levels=None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    streams = pd.read_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "synthetic_click_streams.csv")
    clicks = pd.read_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "synthetic_clicks.csv")
    if levels is not None:
        streams = streams[streams["level"].isin(["human"] + list(levels))]
    return prepare_windows(streams, clicks, window_type)


def real_windows(window_type: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    from .data import trim_moves
    real = load_raw()
    if real.clicks.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame()
    streams = build_click_streams(real)
    return prepare_windows(streams, real.clicks, window_type,
                           moves=trim_moves(real.moves) if len(real.moves) else None)


def fit_final(win: pd.DataFrame, models: list[str], train_levels: list[str], seed: int = C.SEED):
    """Detectors trained on ALL synthetic participants; thresholds from 25% held-out humans."""
    sub = win[(win["level"] == "human") | win["level"].isin(train_levels)]
    hum = sub[sub["level"] == "human"]
    fit_i, cal_i = next(GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=0.25, random_state=seed)
                        .split(hum, groups=hum["participant"]))
    cal = hum.iloc[cal_i]
    fit = pd.concat([hum.iloc[fit_i], sub[sub["level"] != "human"]])
    final = {}
    for m in models:
        det = make_detector(m, seed).fit(fit)
        cs = det.score(cal)
        final[m] = (det, {tg: (0.0 if m in FIXED_RULES else calibrate(cs, tg)) for tg in TARGETS},
                    len(cal))
    return final


def real_track(models: list[str], final: dict) -> str:
    """Evaluation on REAL windows. Returns a markdown section."""
    lines = ["## REAL data", ""]
    rw = real_windows("count")
    rt = real_windows("time")
    for name, w in (("count (50 clicks)", rw), ("time (5 s)", rt)):
        n_h = int((is_attack(w) == 0).sum()) if len(w) else 0
        n_a = int(is_attack(w).sum()) if len(w) else 0
        lines.append(f"- scored real windows, {name}: humans={n_h}, attacks={n_a}")
    lines.append("")
    if rw.empty or len(rw) == 0:
        lines += ["No real window survives cleaning and routing (too few non-held clicks).",
                  "**No real-data detection metric can be computed yet.** The per-event behaviour of the",
                  "original thresholds on the real session is in `data_report.md`.", ""]
        return "\n".join(lines)
    y = is_attack(rw)
    grp_ok = {c: rw.loc[y == k, c].nunique() for c in ("participant", "session_id") for k in (0, 1)}
    if (y == 1).any() and (y == 0).any():
        gc = "participant" if min(rw.loc[y == 0, "participant"].nunique(), rw.loc[y == 1, "participant"].nunique()) >= 2 else "session_id"
        if min(rw.loc[y == 0, gc].nunique(), rw.loc[y == 1, gc].nunique()) >= 2:
            if gc == "session_id":
                lines.append("LIMITATION: fewer than 2 participants per class -> grouping by SESSION; "
                             "results do not measure generalisation to new players.")
            lvls = sorted(set(rw.loc[y == 1, "level"]))
            oof = run_cv(rw, [m for m in models if m != "cnn1d"], lvls, lvls, group_col=gc,
                         n_splits=min(5, rw.loc[y == 0, gc].nunique(), rw.loc[y == 1, gc].nunique()))
            s = summarize(oof, group_col=gc)
            s.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "real_cv_metrics.csv", index=False)
            lines += [md_table(s, ["model", "n_human_windows", "n_attack_windows", "recall@0.01",
                                   "fpr@0.01", "recall@0.001", "fpr@0.001", "pr_auc"])]
    # synthetic-trained detectors applied to real humans
    hum = rw[y == 0]
    rows = []
    for m, (det, thr, ncal) in final.items():
        if m == "cnn1d" or hum.empty:
            continue
        sc = det.score(hum)
        rows.append({"model": m, "real_human_windows": len(hum),
                     **{f"flagged@{tg}": int((sc > thr[tg]).sum()) for tg in TARGETS}})
    if rows:
        lines += ["Synthetic-trained detectors (thresholds from synthetic calibration humans) applied to",
                  "REAL human windows:", "", md_table(pd.DataFrame(rows), list(rows[0].keys()))]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    models = available_models()
    report = ["# Phase D - detection results", "",
              f"**Synthetic sections: {C.DEMO_TAG}.** Humans are placeholder-generator humans "
              "(see README); attack levels: none (auto_fixed), uniform and gaussian jitter (auto_jitter).", ""]
    final = None
    for wt in ("count", "time"):
        win = synthetic_windows(wt, STANDARD_ATTACKS)
        ms = models if wt == "count" else [m for m in models if m not in ("ocsvm", "cnn1d")]
        oof = run_cv(win, ms, STANDARD_ATTACKS, STANDARD_ATTACKS)
        oof.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / f"oof_synthetic_{wt}.csv", index=False)
        s = summarize(oof, levels=STANDARD_ATTACKS)
        s.insert(0, "window_type", wt)
        s.insert(0, "data", "synthetic (DEMO)")
        s.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / f"cv_synthetic_{wt}_metrics.csv", index=False)
        report += [f"## Synthetic, {wt} windows (StratifiedGroupKFold by participant, 5 folds)", "",
                   f"Windows: humans={s['n_human_windows'].iloc[0]}, attacks={s['n_attack_windows'].iloc[0]}; "
                   f"calibration humans per fold ~{s['n_cal_humans_mean'].iloc[0]:.0f} windows "
                   "(an FPR target of 0.1% is close to the resolution limit of this calibration set).", "",
                   md_table(s, ["model", "recall@0.001", "fpr@0.001", "recall@0.01", "fpr@0.01", "pr_auc",
                                "f1@0.01"]),
                   "Recall per attack level at the 1% threshold:", "",
                   md_table(s, ["model"] + [f"recall@0.01[{l}]" for l in STANDARD_ATTACKS]),
                   "Session-level alarm (>= 3 flagged windows in a session, 1% threshold) and confusion counts:", "",
                   md_table(s, ["model", "session_alarm_human", "session_alarm_attack", "tp@0.01", "fp@0.01",
                                "fn@0.01", "tn@0.01"]), ""]
        plot_recall(s, C.FIG_DIR / f"recall_at_fpr_synthetic_{wt}.png",
                    f"Recall at fixed FPR, {wt} windows, 95% participant-bootstrap CI\n{C.DEMO_TAG}")
        plot_pr(oof, C.FIG_DIR / f"pr_curves_synthetic_{wt}.png", f"PR curves, {wt} windows\n{C.DEMO_TAG}")
        if wt == "count":
            final = fit_final(win, [m for m in models if m != "cnn1d"], STANDARD_ATTACKS)
            import joblib
            joblib.dump({m: (d, t, n) for m, (d, t, n) in final.items()},
                        C.PROCESSED_DIR / "final_models_standard.joblib")
            json.dump({m: {str(k): v for k, v in t.items()} for m, (_, t, _) in final.items()},
                      open(C.TAB_DIR / "final_thresholds_standard.json", "w"), indent=2)
    report.append(real_track(models, final))
    (C.RESULTS_DIR / "phase_d_results.md").write_text("\n".join(report), encoding="utf-8")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/explain.py`:

```py
"""Phase G - explainability of the final XGBoost click detector (DEMO model).

* TreeSHAP values (shap library; falls back to XGBoost's native pred_contribs, the same algorithm)
* permutation importance (drop in PR-AUC) on a FRESH synthetic hold-out population
* three example decisions rendered as audit-log lines: what the plugin would write to auditoria.log

    python -m src.explain
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging

import joblib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.inspection import permutation_importance
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score

from . import config as C
from .data import build_click_streams
from .evaluate import prepare_windows
from .features import FEATURES
from .synthetic import build, calibration_from_real
from .train import X_of, is_attack

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DESCRIPTIONS = {
    "mean": "mean click interval (ms)", "std": "std of intervals (ms)", "cv": "coefficient of variation",
    "skew": "skewness", "kurt": "excess kurtosis", "min": "shortest interval (ms)",
    "p05": "5th percentile interval (ms)", "p95": "95th percentile interval (ms)",
    "entropy": "interval histogram entropy (bits)", "ac1": "lag-1 autocorrelation",
    "max_cps_1s": "max clicks in any 1 s", "tick_frac": "fraction of intervals on the 50 ms grid",
    "dup_frac": "fraction of same-tick duplicate swings", "pause_frac": "fraction of pauses > 1 s",
}


def shap_values(model, X: np.ndarray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float, str]:
    try:
        import shap
        ex = shap.TreeExplainer(model)
        sv = ex.shap_values(X)
        base = float(np.ravel(ex.expected_value)[0])
        return np.asarray(sv), base, f"shap {shap.__version__} TreeExplainer"
    except Exception as e:  # version mismatch between shap and xgboost
        import xgboost as xgb
        log.warning("shap failed (%s); using xgboost native TreeSHAP", e)
        contrib = model.get_booster().predict(xgb.DMatrix(X, feature_names=FEATURES), pred_contribs=True)
        return contrib[:, :-1], float(contrib[0, -1]), "xgboost pred_contribs (TreeSHAP)"


def audit_line(row: pd.Series, sv: np.ndarray, score: float, thr: float, human_median: pd.Series,
               k: int = 3) -> str:
    order = np.argsort(-np.abs(sv))[:k]
    reasons = []
    for i in order:
        f = FEATURES[i]
        reasons.append(f"{f}={row[f]:.3g} (typical human {human_median[f]:.3g}) "
                       f"{'raises' if sv[i] > 0 else 'lowers'} suspicion by {abs(sv[i]):.2f} log-odds")
    verdict = "FLAG (+1 VL)" if score > thr else "no action"
    return (f"[ML-AutoClicker] {verdict} | score={score:.3f} threshold={thr:.3f} (1% FPR on calibration "
            f"humans; DEMO model) | window={int(row['n_swings'])} swings, {int(row['n_active'])} active "
            f"intervals, held_frac={row['held_frac']:.2f} | reasons: " + "; ".join(reasons))


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    final = joblib.load(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "final_models_standard.joblib")
    det, thr, _ = final["xgb"]
    model = det.m
    net, hyper, _ = calibration_from_real()
    ds, _ = build(seed=C.SEED + 999, n_humans=16, n_attackers=8, levels=["none", "uniform", "gaussian"],
                  net=net, hyper=hyper)
    w = prepare_windows(build_click_streams(ds), ds.clicks, "count")
    X, y = X_of(w), is_attack(w)
    score = det.score(w)

    sv, base, method = shap_values(model, X)
    imp = pd.DataFrame({"feature": FEATURES, "mean_abs_shap": np.abs(sv).mean(0)})
    pi = permutation_importance(model, X, y, scoring=lambda m, X_, y_: average_precision_score(
        y_, m.predict_proba(X_)[:, 1]), n_repeats=10, random_state=C.SEED, n_jobs=1)
    imp["perm_importance_pr_auc"] = pi.importances_mean
    imp["perm_importance_sd"] = pi.importances_std
    imp["description"] = imp["feature"].map(DESCRIPTIONS)
    imp = imp.sort_values("mean_abs_shap", ascending=False)
    imp.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "explain_feature_importance.csv", index=False)

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(10, 4))
    ax[0].barh(imp["feature"][::-1], imp["mean_abs_shap"][::-1], color="#3b6ea5")
    ax[0].set(xlabel="mean |SHAP| (log-odds)", title=f"XGBoost, {method}")
    ax[1].barh(imp["feature"][::-1], imp["perm_importance_pr_auc"][::-1],
               xerr=imp["perm_importance_sd"][::-1], color="#c0504d")
    ax[1].set(xlabel="drop in PR-AUC when permuted", title="permutation importance (fresh hold-out)")
    for a in ax:
        a.title.set_fontsize(8)
    fig.suptitle(f"Feature importance - {C.DEMO_TAG}", fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(C.FIG_DIR / "explain_feature_importance.png", dpi=130)
    plt.close(fig)

    t1 = thr[0.01]
    human_median = w.loc[y == 0, FEATURES].median()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(C.SEED)
    picks = {
        "attack window flagged with high confidence": np.where((y == 1) & (score > t1))[0],
        "attack window missed (below threshold)": np.where((y == 1) & (score <= t1))[0],
        "human window close to the threshold": np.argsort(np.where(y == 0, np.abs(score - t1), np.inf))[:20],
    }
    lines = [f"# Phase G - explainability ({C.DEMO_TAG})", "",
             f"Model: final XGBoost (trained on synthetic humans + none/uniform/gaussian). Explanation "
             f"method: {method}; base value {base:.3f} log-odds. Evaluated on a fresh synthetic population "
             f"({int((y == 0).sum())} human / {int(y.sum())} attack windows).", "",
             "## Feature ranking", "",
             "| feature | description | mean abs SHAP | permutation importance (PR-AUC drop) |",
             "|---|---|---|---|"]
    lines += [f"| {r.feature} | {r.description} | {r.mean_abs_shap:.3f} | {r.perm_importance_pr_auc:.4f} "
              f"± {r.perm_importance_sd:.4f} |" for r in imp.itertuples()]
    lines += ["", "## Three example decisions as audit-log lines", "",
              "Each line is what `auditoria.log` would contain (actor pseudonymised by HMAC in the plugin).", ""]
    for title, idx in picks.items():
        if len(idx) == 0:
            lines += [f"**{title}:** no such window in this hold-out.", ""]
            continue
        i = int(rng.choice(idx))
        lines += [f"**{title}** (true level: {w.iloc[i]['level']})", "", "```",
                  audit_line(w.iloc[i], sv[i], float(score[i]), t1, human_median), "```", ""]
    (C.RESULTS_DIR / "phase_g_results.md").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    log.info("done (%s)", method)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/export_onnx.py`:

```py
"""Phase H - export the best tree-based model to ONNX for the plugin, plus Java parity fixtures.

The best tree model is chosen by recall at 1% FPR (count windows, Phase D CV table). The exported
model is the final model of evaluate.fit_final (trained on ALL synthetic participants) and its
1%-FPR threshold. It is a DEMO model: the plugin ships it in shadow mode by default.

Outputs
  results/models/click_model.onnx + click_model.json   (copied into plugin resources)
  plugin/src/test/resources/click_features_fixture.json (Python features + scores for Java tests)

    python -m src.export_onnx
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import shutil

import joblib
import numpy as np
import onnxruntime as ort
import pandas as pd

from . import config as C
from .data import clean_click_stream
from .evaluate import TARGETS
from .features import FEATURES, window_features

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def to_onnx(name: str, model):
    from onnxmltools.convert.common.data_types import FloatTensorType
    init = [("features", FloatTensorType([None, len(FEATURES)]))]
    if name == "xgb":
        from onnxmltools import convert_xgboost
        onx = convert_xgboost(model, initial_types=init, target_opset=15)
    else:
        from skl2onnx import convert_sklearn
        from skl2onnx.common.data_types import FloatTensorType as SFT
        onx = convert_sklearn(model, initial_types=[("features", SFT([None, len(FEATURES)]))],
                              options={id(model): {"zipmap": False}}, target_opset=15)
    return onx


def onnx_scores(path, X: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    sess = ort.InferenceSession(str(path), providers=["CPUExecutionProvider"])
    outs = sess.run(None, {sess.get_inputs()[0].name: X.astype(np.float32)})
    prob = [o for o in outs if getattr(o, "ndim", 0) == 2][0]
    return prob[:, 1]


def fixture_cases(rng) -> list[dict]:
    """Hand-made raw event sequences covering dedup, held runs, pauses and same-tick duplicates."""
    cases = []
    def add(name, ev, breaks=()):
        cl = pd.DataFrame({"t_ns": [int(t * 1e6) for t, _ in ev], "source": [s for _, s in ev]})
        br = pd.DataFrame({"t_ns": [int(t * 1e6) for t in breaks]})
        st = clean_click_stream(cl, br)
        f = window_features(st["t_ms"].to_numpy(), st["held"].to_numpy(), st["n_dup"].to_numpy())
        cases.append({"name": name, "events": [[float(t), s] for t, s in ev],
                      "breaks": [float(b) for b in breaks],
                      "accepted_t_ms": st["t_ms"].tolist(), "held": st["held"].astype(bool).tolist(),
                      "n_dup": st["n_dup"].astype(int).tolist(),
                      "features": {k: (None if (isinstance(v, float) and np.isnan(v)) else v)
                                   for k, v in f.items() if k in FEATURES + ["held_frac", "n_active", "valid", "n_swings"]}})
    # 1) fixed 12 CPS clicker observed on the tick grid with jitter
    t = np.cumsum(np.tile([100.0, 50.0, 100.0, 83.0], 13)) + rng.normal(0, 2, 52)
    add("autoclicker_like", [(x, "swing") for x in np.sort(t)])
    # 2) human-like with pauses and interact_air duplicates
    t = np.cumsum(rng.lognormal(np.log(140), 0.4, 60)); t[30:] += 1500
    ev = []
    for x in t:
        ev += [(x - 0.3, "interact_air"), (x, "swing")]
    add("human_like_with_pause", ev)
    # 3) mining runs (instant break then held swings) mixed with clicks
    ev, br = [], []
    base = 0.0
    for k in range(6):
        ev.append((base, "interact_block")); br.append(base + 0.2)
        ev += [(base + 0.6 + 50.0 * j + rng.normal(0, 1.5), "swing") for j in range(6)]
        base += 900
        ev += [(base - 400 + 130 * j, "swing") for j in range(3)]
    add("mining_mixed", sorted(ev), br)
    # 4) same-tick double clicks (duplicates within 15 ms)
    t = np.cumsum(np.tile([50.0, 50.0, 100.0], 20))
    ev = []
    for x in t:
        ev += [(x, "swing"), (x + 0.2, "swing")]
    add("same_tick_doubles", ev)
    return cases


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    cv = pd.read_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "cv_synthetic_count_metrics.csv")
    trees = cv[cv["model"].isin(["rf", "xgb"])].sort_values(["recall@0.01", "pr_auc"], ascending=False)
    final = joblib.load(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "final_models_standard.joblib")
    # Selection rule: highest recall at 1% FPR; if the runner-up is within 1 percentage point, take
    # the model with the smaller ONNX graph (server impact / TPS is a first-class requirement).
    sizes = {m: len(to_onnx(m, final[m][0].m).SerializeToString()) for m in trees["model"]}
    best = trees["model"].iloc[0]
    if len(trees) > 1 and trees["recall@0.01"].iloc[0] - trees["recall@0.01"].iloc[1] < 0.01:
        best = min(trees["model"].iloc[:2], key=sizes.get)
    log.info("recall@1%%: %s; onnx bytes: %s -> selected %s",
             dict(zip(trees["model"], trees["recall@0.01"].round(4))), sizes, best)
    det, thr, ncal = final[best]
    onx = to_onnx(best, det.m)
    out = C.MODEL_DIR / "click_model.onnx"
    out.write_bytes(onx.SerializeToString())

    # parity python vs onnxruntime on real-shaped windows
    from .evaluate import synthetic_windows
    w = synthetic_windows("count", ["none", "uniform", "gaussian"]).sample(2000, random_state=C.SEED)
    X = w[FEATURES].to_numpy(np.float32)
    py, ox = det.score(w), onnx_scores(out, X)
    maxdiff = float(np.max(np.abs(py - ox)))
    log.info("python vs onnx max |diff| = %.2e", maxdiff)
    if maxdiff > 1e-4:
        raise SystemExit(f"ONNX parity failed: {maxdiff}")

    row = cv[cv["model"] == best].iloc[0]
    meta = {
        "model": best, "features": FEATURES, "threshold_fpr_1pct": thr[0.01],
        "threshold_fpr_0.1pct": thr[0.001], "calibration_human_windows": int(ncal),
        "window_clicks": C.WINDOW_CLICKS, "stride_clicks": C.STRIDE_CLICKS,
        "min_intervals": C.MIN_INTERVALS_PER_WINDOW, "dedup_ms": C.DEDUP_MS,
        "held_max_iv_ms": C.HELD_MAX_IV_MS, "fresh_dig_ms": C.FRESH_DIG_MS, "pause_ms": C.PAUSE_MS,
        "held_route_frac": C.HELD_ROUTE_FRAC, "tick_ms": C.TICK_MS, "tick_near_ms": C.TICK_NEAR_MS,
        "trained_on": "SYNTHETIC humans (placeholder generator) + synthetic none/uniform/gaussian clickers",
        "demo": True, "warning": C.DEMO_TAG,
        "cv_recall_at_1pct_fpr_synthetic": float(row["recall@0.01"]),
        "cv_fpr_at_1pct_synthetic": float(row["fpr@0.01"]),
        "onnx_parity_max_abs_diff": maxdiff,
        "selection": {"recall_at_1pct": dict(zip(trees["model"], trees["recall@0.01"].astype(float))),
                      "onnx_bytes": sizes,
                      "rule": "best recall@1%FPR; within 1 point -> smaller ONNX model"},
    }
    (C.MODEL_DIR / "click_model.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=2))
    res = C.PLUGIN_RES_DIR / "models"
    res.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copy(out, res / "click_model.onnx")
    shutil.copy(C.MODEL_DIR / "click_model.json", res / "click_model.json")

    # Java parity fixtures: features of hand-made cases + model scores of sample windows
    rng = np.random.default_rng(C.SEED)
    # 25 windows spread over the whole score range (not only saturated 0/1 scores)
    order = np.argsort(py)
    sample = w.iloc[order[np.linspace(0, len(order) - 1, 25).astype(int)]]
    fx = {"constants": {k: meta[k] for k in ("dedup_ms", "held_max_iv_ms", "fresh_dig_ms", "pause_ms",
                                              "tick_ms", "tick_near_ms", "min_intervals")},
          "features": FEATURES, "cases": fixture_cases(rng),
          "model_samples": [{"x": [float(v) for v in r], "score": float(s)}
                            for r, s in zip(sample[FEATURES].to_numpy(np.float32), det.score(sample))]}
    tdir = C.REPO_DIR / "plugin" / "src" / "test" / "resources"
    tdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (tdir / "click_features_fixture.json").write_text(json.dumps(fx, indent=1))
    log.info("exported %s (%d bytes), threshold@1%%=%.4f", out.name, out.stat().st_size, thr[0.01])

    # Latency of one window (feature extraction in Python + single-row ONNX inference, 1 thread).
