```py
    # Indicative only: measured in Python on this machine, not inside the Minecraft server.
    import platform
    import time
    so = ort.SessionOptions(); so.intra_op_num_threads = 1
    sess = ort.InferenceSession(str(out), so, providers=["CPUExecutionProvider"])
    name = sess.get_inputs()[0].name
    x1 = X[:1]
    for _ in range(200):
        sess.run(None, {name: x1})
    lat = []
    for i in range(2000):
        t0 = time.perf_counter(); sess.run(None, {name: X[i % len(X):i % len(X) + 1]}); lat.append(time.perf_counter() - t0)
    tw = np.cumsum(np.full(50, 100.0)); hw = np.zeros(50, bool); dw = np.zeros(50)
    fl = []
    for _ in range(2000):
        t0 = time.perf_counter(); window_features(tw, hw, dw); fl.append(time.perf_counter() - t0)
    pd.DataFrame([{"what": "onnx_inference_1_window", "median_us": np.median(lat) * 1e6,
                   "p99_us": np.percentile(lat, 99) * 1e6},
                  {"what": "python_feature_extraction_1_window", "median_us": np.median(fl) * 1e6,
                   "p99_us": np.percentile(fl, 99) * 1e6}]).assign(
        machine=f"{platform.processor() or platform.machine()} / {platform.system()}",
        note="Python onnxruntime, 1 intra-op thread; indicative, not measured in the server").to_csv(
        C.TAB_DIR / "onnx_latency.csv", index=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/features.py`:

```py
"""Phase C - window features.

Feature definitions are written out explicitly (no library defaults) because the Java plugin
re-implements them (ClickFeatureExtractor.java) and a parity test compares both on fixtures.

Window input: consecutive ACCEPTED swings of one session (after de-duplication) with their
held-button flag and duplicate count (see data.clean_click_stream).
  intervals  = diffs between consecutive swings of the window, keeping interval i only when
               swing i is not held (held-button mining swings are game-generated, not player clicks)
  pause      = kept interval > PAUSE_MS (excluded from distribution statistics)
  A          = kept intervals <= PAUSE_MS (used for every distribution statistic)
A window is valid when len(A) >= MIN_INTERVALS_PER_WINDOW. A valid window with
held_frac >= HELD_ROUTE_FRAC is routed to "mining" and is NOT scored by click detectors.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as C

# Model inputs. held_frac, n_active, ping/tps are kept for routing/audit only (see README).
FEATURES = ["mean", "std", "cv", "skew", "kurt", "min", "p05", "p95", "entropy", "ac1",
            "max_cps_1s", "tick_frac", "dup_frac", "pause_frac"]
AUDIT = ["n_swings", "n_active", "held_frac", "ping_mean", "tps_min"]
N_HIST_BINS = len(C.HIST_EDGES_MS)  # 20 regular bins + 1 overflow bin


def percentile_linear(sorted_a: np.ndarray, q: float) -> float:
    """numpy 'linear' percentile, written explicitly (Java parity)."""
    n = len(sorted_a)
    pos = q * (n - 1)
    lo = int(math.floor(pos))
    hi = min(lo + 1, n - 1)
    return float(sorted_a[lo] + (pos - lo) * (sorted_a[hi] - sorted_a[lo]))


def interval_stats(A: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float)
    n = len(A)
    mean = float(A.mean())
    d = A - mean
    m2 = float(np.mean(d ** 2)); m3 = float(np.mean(d ** 3)); m4 = float(np.mean(d ** 4))
    std = math.sqrt(m2)
    skew = m3 / m2 ** 1.5 if m2 > 1e-12 else 0.0
    kurt = m4 / m2 ** 2 - 3.0 if m2 > 1e-12 else 0.0
    s = np.sort(A)
    bins = np.minimum(np.floor(A / 25.0).astype(int), N_HIST_BINS - 1)
    p = np.bincount(bins, minlength=N_HIST_BINS) / n
    p = p[p > 0]
    entropy = float(-(p * np.log2(p)).sum())
    den = float((d ** 2).sum())
    ac1 = float((d[:-1] * d[1:]).sum() / den) if den > 1e-12 and n > 1 else 0.0
    res = A - C.TICK_MS * np.round(A / C.TICK_MS)
    return {"mean": mean, "std": std, "cv": std / mean if mean > 0 else 0.0, "skew": skew,
            "kurt": kurt, "min": float(s[0]), "p05": percentile_linear(s, 0.05),
            "p95": percentile_linear(s, 0.95), "entropy": entropy, "ac1": ac1,
            "tick_frac": float(np.mean(np.abs(res) <= C.TICK_NEAR_MS))}


def max_clicks_in_1s(t_ms: np.ndarray) -> int:
    t = np.sort(np.asarray(t_ms, dtype=float))
    best, j = 0, 0
    for i in range(len(t)):
        while t[i] - t[j] >= 1000.0:
            j += 1
        best = max(best, i - j + 1)
    return best


def window_features(t_ms: np.ndarray, held: np.ndarray, n_dup: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    t = np.asarray(t_ms, dtype=float)
    held = np.asarray(held, dtype=bool)
    n_dup = np.asarray(n_dup, dtype=float)
    n = len(t)
    out = {"n_swings": n, "held_frac": float(held.mean()) if n else float("nan"),
           "dup_frac": float(n_dup.sum() / (n + n_dup.sum())) if n else float("nan")}
    diffs = np.diff(t)
    kept = diffs[~held[1:]] if n > 1 else np.array([])
    A = kept[kept <= C.PAUSE_MS]
    out["pause_frac"] = float(np.mean(kept > C.PAUSE_MS)) if len(kept) else float("nan")
    out["n_active"] = int(len(A))
    out["valid"] = len(A) >= C.MIN_INTERVALS_PER_WINDOW
    if out["valid"]:
        out.update(interval_stats(A))
        out["max_cps_1s"] = float(max_clicks_in_1s(t[~held]))
    else:
        out.update({k: float("nan") for k in FEATURES if k not in out})
    out["routed"] = "mining" if out["held_frac"] >= C.HELD_ROUTE_FRAC else "click"
    return out


def _window_bounds(t: np.ndarray, window_type: str):
    if window_type == "count":
        n = len(t)
        for s in range(0, n - C.WINDOW_CLICKS + 1, C.STRIDE_CLICKS):
            yield s, s + C.WINDOW_CLICKS
    elif window_type == "time":
        if len(t) == 0:
            return
        w, st = C.WINDOW_S * 1000, C.STRIDE_S * 1000
        start = t[0]
        while start + w <= t[-1] + 1e-9:
            i0 = int(np.searchsorted(t, start, "left"))
            i1 = int(np.searchsorted(t, start + w, "left"))
            yield i0, i1
            start += st
    else:
        raise ValueError(window_type)


def make_windows(streams: pd.DataFrame, window_type: str = "count",
                 moves: pd.DataFrame | None = None, keep_sequences: bool = False) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Windows for every session in a cleaned stream frame (output of data.build_click_streams)."""
    rows = []
    mv = {sid: g for sid, g in moves.groupby("session_id")} if moves is not None and len(moves) else {}
    meta_cols = [c for c in ["session_id", "participant", "label", "activity", "origin", "level"]
                 if c in streams.columns]
    for sid, g in streams.groupby("session_id", sort=False):
        g = g.sort_values("t_ms")
        t = g["t_ms"].to_numpy(); h = g["held"].to_numpy(bool); d = g["n_dup"].to_numpy()
        meta = g.iloc[0][meta_cols].to_dict()
        m = mv.get(sid)
        for w, (i0, i1) in enumerate(_window_bounds(t, window_type)):
            f = window_features(t[i0:i1], h[i0:i1], d[i0:i1])
            f.update(meta)
            f.update({"window_type": window_type, "window_idx": w,
                      "t_start_ms": float(t[i0]) if i1 > i0 else float("nan"),
                      "t_end_ms": float(t[i1 - 1]) if i1 > i0 else float("nan")})
            if m is not None and i1 > i0:
                mm = m[(m["t_ns"] >= t[i0] * 1e6) & (m["t_ns"] <= t[i1 - 1] * 1e6)]
                f["ping_mean"] = float(mm["ping"].mean()) if len(mm) else float("nan")
                f["tps_min"] = float(mm["tps"].min()) if len(mm) else float("nan")
            else:
                f["ping_mean"] = f["tps_min"] = float("nan")
            if keep_sequences:
                diffs = np.diff(t[i0:i1])
                f["seq"] = diffs[~h[i0 + 1:i1]].astype(np.float32)
            rows.append(f)
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def scored(windows: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Windows that click detectors score: valid and not routed to mining."""
    return windows[windows["valid"].astype(bool) & (windows["routed"] == "click")].reset_index(drop=True)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ movement (report only)
def movement_windows(moves: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """5 s windows of (already trimmed) moves: speed, acceleration and airborne ratio."""
    rows = []
    for sid, g in moves.groupby("session_id"):
        g = g.sort_values("t_ns")
        t = g["t_ns"].to_numpy() / 1e6
        hspd = np.hypot(g["dx"].to_numpy(), g["dz"].to_numpy())
        if len(t) == 0:
            continue
        start = t[0]
        while start + C.WINDOW_S * 1000 <= t[-1] + 1e-9:
            m = (t >= start) & (t < start + C.WINDOW_S * 1000)
            if m.sum() >= 5:
                h = hspd[m]
                rows.append({"session_id": sid, "label": g["label"].iloc[0], "t_start_ms": start,
                             "n": int(m.sum()), "speed_mean": float(h.mean()),
                             "speed_p95": float(np.percentile(h, 95)), "speed_max": float(h.max()),
                             "accel_abs_mean": float(np.abs(np.diff(h)).mean()) if m.sum() > 1 else 0.0,
                             "airborne_ratio": float((g["on_ground"].to_numpy()[m] == 0).mean()),
                             "dy_abs_max": float(np.abs(g["dy"].to_numpy()[m]).max()),
                             "ping_mean": float(g["ping"].to_numpy()[m].mean()),
                             "tps_min": float(g["tps"].to_numpy()[m].min())})
            start += C.STRIDE_S * 1000
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ script
def _summary(win: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if win.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame()
    g = win.groupby(["origin", "window_type", "level", "label"])
    out = g.agg(windows=("valid", "size"), valid=("valid", "sum"),
                routed_mining=("routed", lambda r: int((r == "mining").sum())),
                participants=("participant", "nunique")).reset_index()
    sc = scored(win)
    if len(sc):
        out = out.merge(sc.groupby(["origin", "window_type", "level", "label"])
                        .agg(scored=("valid", "size"), **{f"median_{f}": (f, "median") for f in
                                                          ["mean", "cv", "entropy", "ac1", "tick_frac", "max_cps_1s"]})
                        .reset_index(), how="left")
    return out


def plot_features(win: pd.DataFrame, path) -> None:
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    sc = scored(win[win["window_type"] == "count"])
    levels = ["human", "none", "uniform", "gaussian", "human_like"]
    feats = ["cv", "entropy", "ac1", "tick_frac", "dup_frac", "max_cps_1s"]
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, len(feats), figsize=(3.0 * len(feats), 3.2))
    for ax, f in zip(axes, feats):
        data = [sc.loc[(sc["level"] == l) & (sc["origin"] == "synthetic"), f].dropna() for l in levels]
        ax.boxplot(data, tick_labels=levels, showfliers=False)
        ax.set_title(f, fontsize=9)
        ax.tick_params(axis="x", rotation=60, labelsize=7)
    fig.suptitle(f"Window features (50-click windows) by level - {C.DEMO_TAG}", fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=120)
    plt.close(fig)


def main() -> None:
    import logging
    from .data import build_click_streams, load_raw, trim_moves
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    log = logging.getLogger("features")
    syn = pd.read_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "synthetic_click_streams.csv")
    real = load_raw()
    rs = build_click_streams(real)
    mv = trim_moves(real.moves) if len(real.moves) else None
    allw = []
    for wt in ("count", "time"):
        sw = make_windows(syn, wt)
        sw.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / f"synthetic_windows_{wt}.csv", index=False)
        rw = make_windows(rs, wt, moves=mv) if len(rs) else pd.DataFrame()
        rw.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / f"real_windows_{wt}.csv", index=False)
        allw += [sw, rw]
        log.info("%s windows: synthetic=%d real=%d (real scored=%d)", wt, len(sw), len(rw),
                 len(scored(rw)) if len(rw) else 0)
    win = pd.concat([w for w in allw if len(w)], ignore_index=True)
    _summary(win).to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "window_summary.csv", index=False)
    plot_features(win, C.FIG_DIR / "synthetic_feature_boxplots.png")
    if mv is not None and len(mv):
        mw = movement_windows(mv)
        mw.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "real_movement_windows.csv", index=False)
        if len(mw):
            mw.drop(columns=["session_id", "t_start_ms"]).groupby("label").agg(["count", "median", "max"]) \
              .T.reset_index().to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "real_movement_window_summary.csv", index=False)
        log.info("real movement windows: %d", len(mw))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/lag.py`:

```py
"""Phase F - robustness to network lag (SIMULATED; no tc netem / clumsy run here).

Lag is simulated on top of the recorded/observed server timestamps:
  * per-packet extra jitter  ~ Normal(0, sigma) with sigma = JITTER_FRAC * RTT
  * lag spikes (Poisson, rate per second) during which delivery freezes and queued packets are
    released together (bunching), duration ~ U(0.5, 1.5) * RTT
  * TCP order preserved
The mapping from ping class to (sigma, spike rate) is an ASSUMPTION (table PING_CLASSES), not a
measurement. docs/real_lag_test_protocol.md describes how to replace it with real measurements.

Evaluated:
  DEMO   fresh synthetic humans (new seed, never seen in training) and standard attackers, scored by the
         final Phase-D detectors with their clean-data thresholds -> FPR / recall per ping class.
  REAL   the real sessions' timestamps with simulated lag (N_REPEATS draws) -> per-event flags of
         the original thesis rule (naive and cleaned), because no real window is scoreable yet.

    python -m src.lag
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging

import joblib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as C
from .data import Dataset, build_click_streams, load_raw, naive_intervals
from .evaluate import TARGETS, md_table, prepare_windows
from .observation import enforce_order, lag_spikes
from .synthetic import build, calibration_from_real

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
JITTER_FRAC = 0.10
PING_CLASSES = {  # ping (RTT, ms) -> (extra jitter sd ms, spikes per second)
    0: (0.0, 0.0),
    20: (JITTER_FRAC * 20, 0.01),
    80: (JITTER_FRAC * 80, 0.03),
    150: (JITTER_FRAC * 150, 0.06),
    250: (JITTER_FRAC * 250, 0.12),
}
N_REPEATS = 20


def lag_session(t_ms: np.ndarray, rng, ping: int) -> np.ndarray:
    sigma, rate = PING_CLASSES[ping]
    if ping == 0:
        return t_ms
    order = np.argsort(t_ms, kind="stable")
    s = t_ms[order] + rng.normal(0, sigma, len(t_ms))
    s = enforce_order(s)
    s = lag_spikes(s, rng, rate, (0.5 * ping, 1.5 * ping))
    out = np.empty_like(s)
    out[order] = s
    return out


def apply_lag(ds: Dataset, rng, ping: int) -> Dataset:
    """Re-time every click and break event of every session (one shared queue per session)."""
    cl, br = ds.clicks.copy(), ds.breaks.copy()
    cl["_k"], br["_k"] = "c", "b"
    allev = pd.concat([cl[["session_id", "t_ns", "_k"]].assign(_i=cl.index),
                       br[["session_id", "t_ns", "_k"]].assign(_i=br.index)], ignore_index=True)
    new = np.empty(len(allev))
    for _, idx in allev.groupby("session_id").groups.items():
        idx = np.asarray(idx)
        new[idx] = lag_session(allev.loc[idx, "t_ns"].to_numpy() / 1e6, rng, ping)
    allev["t_new"] = (new * 1e6).astype("int64")
    m = allev.set_index(["_k", "_i"])["t_new"]
    cl["t_ns"] = m.loc["c"].reindex(cl.index).to_numpy()
    if len(br):
        br["t_ns"] = m.loc["b"].reindex(br.index).to_numpy()
    cl = cl.drop(columns="_k").sort_values(["session_id", "t_ns"], kind="stable")
    br = br.drop(columns="_k")
    return Dataset(cl, ds.moves, br, ds.sessions, ds.issues)


def demo_lag(final: dict, rng) -> pd.DataFrame:
    net, hyper, _ = calibration_from_real()
    ds, _ = build(seed=C.SEED + 777, n_humans=20, n_attackers=8, levels=["none", "uniform", "gaussian"],
                  net=net, hyper=hyper)
    rows = []
    for ping in PING_CLASSES:
        d = apply_lag(ds, rng, ping)
        w = prepare_windows(build_click_streams(d), d.clicks, "count")
        hum = w["level"] == "human"
        for m, (det, thr, _) in final.items():
            sc = det.score(w)
            for tg in TARGETS:
                f = sc > thr[tg]
                rows.append({"data": "synthetic (DEMO)", "ping_ms": ping, "model": m, "target_fpr": tg,
                             "human_windows": int(hum.sum()), "fpr": float(f[hum].mean()),
                             "attack_windows": int((~hum).sum()), "recall": float(f[~hum].mean())})
        log.info("ping %d done", ping)
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def real_lag(rng) -> pd.DataFrame:
    real = load_raw()
    if real.clicks.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame()
    rows = []
    thr = C.ORIG_CLICK_MIN_DIFF_MS
    for ping in PING_CLASSES:
        for rep in range(N_REPEATS if ping else 1):
            d = apply_lag(real, rng, ping)
            st = build_click_streams(d)
            nv = naive_intervals(d.clicks, {"interact_air", "interact_block"})["iv_ms"].to_numpy()
            act = np.floor(st.loc[~st["held"].astype(bool), "iv_ms"].dropna().to_numpy())
            rows.append({"data": "REAL timestamps + SIMULATED lag", "ping_ms": ping, "repeat": rep,
                         "naive_java_flags": int(np.sum((nv > 0) & (nv < thr))), "naive_java_n": len(nv),
                         "cleaned_active_flags": int(np.sum((act > 0) & (act < thr))),
                         "cleaned_active_n": len(act), "held_swings": int(st["held"].sum())})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def plot(demo: pd.DataFrame, path):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    d = demo[demo["target_fpr"] == 0.01]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(10, 3.6))
    for m, g in d.groupby("model", sort=False):
        ls = "--" if m.startswith("baseline") else "-"
        ax[0].plot(g["ping_ms"], g["fpr"], marker="o", ls=ls, label=m)
        ax[1].plot(g["ping_ms"], g["recall"], marker="o", ls=ls, label=m)
    ax[0].axhline(0.01, color="grey", lw=0.8, ls=":")
    ax[0].set(xlabel="simulated ping class (ms)", ylabel="human FPR (window)", yscale="symlog",
              title="human FPR, threshold calibrated at 1% on clean data")
    ax[1].set(xlabel="simulated ping class (ms)", ylabel="recall (standard attackers)", ylim=(-0.02, 1.02),
              title="recall under the same lag")
    for a in ax:
        a.title.set_fontsize(8); a.grid(alpha=0.3)
    ax[1].legend(fontsize=7)
    fig.suptitle(f"SIMULATED lag - {C.DEMO_TAG}", fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=130)
    plt.close(fig)


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(C.SEED + 2)
    final = joblib.load(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "final_models_standard.joblib")
    demo = demo_lag(final, rng)
    demo.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "lag_synthetic.csv", index=False)
    plot(demo, C.FIG_DIR / "lag_fpr_synthetic.png")
    real = real_lag(rng)
    rep = [f"# Phase F - lag robustness (SIMULATED lag)", "",
           "Ping class -> (extra per-packet jitter sd, lag spikes per second) is an assumption: " +
           ", ".join(f"{p} ms -> ({s:.0f} ms, {r}/s)" for p, (s, r) in PING_CLASSES.items()) +
           "; spike duration U(0.5, 1.5) x ping, packets released together after a spike.", "",
           f"## Synthetic ({C.DEMO_TAG}) - fresh human population never used for training", "",
           md_table(demo[demo["target_fpr"] == 0.01].pivot(index="model", columns="ping_ms", values="fpr")
                    .reset_index().rename(columns=lambda c: f"fpr@ping{c}" if c != "model" else c),
                    ["model"] + [f"fpr@ping{p}" for p in PING_CLASSES], fmt="{:.4f}"),
           "Recall on standard attackers under the same lag (1% threshold):", "",
           md_table(demo[demo["target_fpr"] == 0.01].pivot(index="model", columns="ping_ms", values="recall")
                    .reset_index().rename(columns=lambda c: f"recall@ping{c}" if c != "model" else c),
                    ["model"] + [f"recall@ping{p}" for p in PING_CLASSES]), ""]
    if len(real):
        real.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "lag_real_events.csv", index=False)
        agg = real.groupby("ping_ms").agg(repeats=("repeat", "size"),
                                         naive_java_flags_mean=("naive_java_flags", "mean"),
                                         naive_java_n=("naive_java_n", "mean"),
                                         cleaned_active_flags_mean=("cleaned_active_flags", "mean"),
                                         cleaned_active_n=("cleaned_active_n", "mean"),
                                         held_swings_mean=("held_swings", "mean")).reset_index()
        agg.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "lag_real_events_summary.csv", index=False)
        rep += ["## REAL session timestamps + SIMULATED lag - original rule, per event", "",
                "Every flag is a false positive (the session is human). One short session: anecdotal.", "",
                md_table(agg, list(agg.columns), fmt="{:.2f}")]
    (C.RESULTS_DIR / "phase_f_results.md").write_text("\n".join(rep), encoding="utf-8")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/observation.py`:

```py
"""Server-side observation model shared by every synthetic generator (humans and adversaries).

A "true" click time t (what the finger/clicker does) becomes a server event in three steps:

1. Client tick quantization: the Java client handles mouse input once per client tick (20 Hz),
   so a click is sent at the next client tick boundary  c = phase + 50 * ceil((t - phase) / 50).
   Several clicks inside the same client tick are sent back-to-back (sub-millisecond apart).
   This is consistent with the REAL data: accepted swing intervals cluster at multiples of
   50 ms (see results/data_report.md). It is a model, not a measured client trace.
2. Network + server processing jitter: s = c + base_delay + J, J ~ Normal(0, sigma_ms).
   sigma_ms is calibrated from the real interval residuals when there are enough of them
   (an interval residual is the difference of two independent jitters -> sd / sqrt(2)).
3. TCP keeps order: s_i = max(s_i, s_{i-1} + 0.05 ms).

Lag spikes (lag.py) are added on top with `lag_spikes`.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from . import config as C

DEFAULT_SIGMA_MS = 2.5   # placeholder when there is not enough real data to calibrate
MIN_CALIBRATION_N = 30


@dataclass
class NetParams:
    sigma_ms: float = DEFAULT_SIGMA_MS
    base_ms: float = 0.0
    source: str = "placeholder"


def calibrate_jitter(real_iv_ms: np.ndarray) -> NetParams:
    """Single-event jitter sd from real interval residuals to the 50 ms grid (robust MAD)."""
    from .data import tick_residuals
    iv = np.asarray(real_iv_ms, dtype=float)
    iv = iv[(iv > 0) & (iv < C.PAUSE_MS)]
    if len(iv) < MIN_CALIBRATION_N:
        return NetParams()
    r = tick_residuals(iv)
    r = r[np.abs(r) <= 15]                      # ignore intervals that are not grid-like at all
    if len(r) < MIN_CALIBRATION_N:
        return NetParams()
    mad_sd = 1.4826 * np.median(np.abs(r - np.median(r)))
    return NetParams(sigma_ms=float(max(mad_sd / np.sqrt(2), 0.5)), source=f"real (n={len(r)})")


def client_quantize(t_ms: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
    phase = rng.uniform(0, C.TICK_MS)
    k = np.ceil((np.asarray(t_ms) - phase) / C.TICK_MS)
    c = phase + C.TICK_MS * k
    # clicks in the same tick are sent back-to-back
    same = np.r_[False, np.diff(c) == 0]
    run = np.zeros(len(c))
    for i in range(1, len(c)):
        run[i] = run[i - 1] + 1 if same[i] else 0
    return c + run * rng.uniform(0.05, 0.4, size=len(c))


def network(c_ms: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator, net: NetParams) -> np.ndarray:
    s = np.asarray(c_ms, dtype=float) + net.base_ms + rng.normal(0, net.sigma_ms, size=len(c_ms))
    return enforce_order(s)


def enforce_order(s: np.ndarray, gap_ms: float = 0.05) -> np.ndarray:
    s = np.array(s, dtype=float)
    for i in range(1, len(s)):
        if s[i] < s[i - 1] + gap_ms:
            s[i] = s[i - 1] + gap_ms
    return s


def lag_spikes(s_ms: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator, rate_per_s: float,
               dur_ms: tuple[float, float]) -> np.ndarray:
    """Freeze delivery during random spikes and release queued packets together (bunching)."""
    s = np.array(s_ms, dtype=float)
    if rate_per_s <= 0 or len(s) == 0:
        return s
    T = s[-1] - s[0] + 1
    n = rng.poisson(rate_per_s * T / 1000.0)
    for start in rng.uniform(s[0], s[-1], size=n):
        end = start + rng.uniform(*dur_ms)
        m = (s >= start) & (s < end)
        s[m] = end + np.arange(m.sum()) * 0.05
    return enforce_order(np.sort(s))


def observe(t_ms: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator, net: NetParams) -> np.ndarray:
    """True click times (sorted, ms) -> server receive times (ms)."""
    if len(t_ms) == 0:
        return np.asarray(t_ms, dtype=float)
    return network(client_quantize(np.sort(t_ms), rng), rng, net)

```

`ml/src/synth_humans.py`:

```py
"""Phase B - synthetic HUMAN click sessions (always origin = synthetic).

Two modes:
* "fitted": used automatically when the real data contain at least MIN_FIT_INTERVALS active
  (non-held) click intervals < 1 s from human sessions. Per-participant lognormal parameters,
  lag-1 autocorrelation and pause rate are estimated from the real data and synthetic
  participants are drawn around them.
* "placeholder": otherwise. A lognormal-with-structure generator whose hyper-parameters are
  ASSUMPTIONS (listed in PLACEHOLDER_HYPER), not measurements. Everything trained or evaluated
  on these humans is a DEMO and says so.

The generator returns true click times; the shared observation model (observation.py) turns them
into what the server would record, including client-tick quantization.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as C
from .observation import NetParams, observe

MIN_FIT_INTERVALS = 300

# Placeholder hyper-distribution (uniform ranges). Assumptions, documented in README.
PLACEHOLDER_HYPER = {
    "median_ms": (90.0, 170.0),     # ~6-11 CPS while actively clicking
    "sigma": (0.20, 0.45),          # log-sd of intervals
    "rho": (0.10, 0.50),            # AR(1) of log-interval deviations (rhythm drift)
    "bout_mean_clicks": (8.0, 25.0),
    "pause_median_ms": (400.0, 1200.0),
    "fatigue": (0.0, 0.15),         # relative slow-down of the median over a session
}


@dataclass
class HumanParams:
    median_ms: float
    sigma: float
    rho: float
    bout_mean_clicks: float
    pause_median_ms: float
    fatigue: float


def sample_params(rng: np.random.Generator, hyper: dict = PLACEHOLDER_HYPER) -> HumanParams:
    return HumanParams(**{k: float(rng.uniform(*v)) for k, v in hyper.items()})


def fit_hyper(real_streams: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[dict | None, dict]:
    """Estimate the hyper-distribution from real human data, or (None, info) if not enough."""
    h = real_streams[real_streams["label"].isin(C.HUMAN_LABELS) & ~real_streams["held"].astype(bool)]
    iv = h["iv_ms"].dropna()
    act = h[(h["iv_ms"] > 40) & (h["iv_ms"] < C.PAUSE_MS)]
    info = {"active_intervals": int(len(act)), "participants": int(h["participant"].nunique()),
            "min_required": MIN_FIT_INTERVALS}
    if len(act) < MIN_FIT_INTERVALS:
        return None, info
    rows = []
    for _, g in act.groupby("participant"):
        x = np.log(g["iv_ms"].to_numpy())
        rows.append({"median_ms": float(np.exp(np.median(x))), "sigma": float(np.std(x, ddof=1)),
                     "rho": float(np.corrcoef(x[:-1], x[1:])[0, 1]) if len(x) > 3 else 0.3})
    f = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    pause_frac = float((iv >= C.PAUSE_MS).mean()) if len(iv) else 0.05
    def rng_of(col, lo, hi):
        m, s = f[col].mean(), (f[col].std(ddof=1) if len(f) > 1 else abs(f[col].mean()) * 0.15)
        return (float(max(lo, m - 1.5 * s)), float(min(hi, m + 1.5 * s)))
    hyper = dict(PLACEHOLDER_HYPER)
    hyper["median_ms"] = rng_of("median_ms", 45, 400)
    hyper["sigma"] = rng_of("sigma", 0.05, 1.0)
    hyper["rho"] = rng_of("rho", -0.5, 0.9)
    bout = 1.0 / max(pause_frac, 1e-3)
    hyper["bout_mean_clicks"] = (0.7 * bout, 1.3 * bout)
    info.update({"fitted_per_participant": rows})
    return hyper, info


def human_click_times(p: HumanParams, duration_s: float, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
    """True click times (ms) of one combat-style session."""
    T = duration_s * 1000.0
    t, out, dev = rng.uniform(0, 1000), [], 0.0
    clicks_left = rng.geometric(1.0 / p.bout_mean_clicks)
    while t < T:
        out.append(t)
        clicks_left -= 1
        if clicks_left <= 0:
            t += float(rng.lognormal(np.log(p.pause_median_ms), 0.6))
            clicks_left = rng.geometric(1.0 / p.bout_mean_clicks)
            dev = 0.0
            continue
        dev = p.rho * dev + np.sqrt(1 - p.rho ** 2) * rng.normal(0, p.sigma)
        med = p.median_ms * (1 + p.fatigue * t / T)
        t += max(35.0, med * np.exp(dev))  # physiological floor for one finger
    return np.asarray(out)


def mining_events(duration_s: float, rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Held-button mining in TRUE time: (dig_start_times, held_swing_times, break_times).

    While the button is held the client sends one swing per client tick. Dig durations are
    assumptions (1-12 ticks, i.e. instant-ish to slow blocks); between digs the player either keeps
    holding (next block starts at the next tick) or releases for a lognormal gap.
    """
    T = duration_s * 1000.0
    t = rng.uniform(0, 1000)
    starts, swings, brk = [], [], []
    while t < T:
        starts.append(t)
        ticks = int(rng.integers(1, 13))
        sw = t + C.TICK_MS * np.arange(ticks + 1)
        swings.extend(sw.tolist())
        brk.append(sw[-1] + 1.0)
        if rng.random() < 0.6:
            t = sw[-1] + C.TICK_MS
        else:
            t = sw[-1] + float(rng.lognormal(np.log(350), 0.5))
    return np.asarray(starts), np.asarray(swings), np.asarray(brk)


def to_recorder_frames(session_id: str, label: str, swings_ms: np.ndarray,
                       rng: np.random.Generator, net: NetParams,
                       dig_starts_ms: np.ndarray | None = None,
                       breaks_ms: np.ndarray | None = None) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Apply the observation model and emit clicks.csv/breaks.csv-shaped frames.

    All events of a session go through ONE observation call (same tick phase, order kept).
    Combat clicks (no dig) are preceded by an interact_air event (assumption: the server-side
    ray trace misses a block); dig starts are an interact_block just before the first swing.
    """
    mining = dig_starts_ms is not None and len(dig_starts_ms) > 0
    ev = [(t, 3, "swing") for t in np.asarray(swings_ms)]
    if mining:
        ev += [(t - 0.5, 0, "interact_block") for t in dig_starts_ms]
    else:
        ev += [(t - 0.3, 1, "interact_air") for t in np.asarray(swings_ms)]
    if breaks_ms is not None:
        ev += [(t, 2, "break") for t in breaks_ms]
    ev.sort(key=lambda e: (e[0], e[1]))
    obs = observe(np.array([e[0] for e in ev]), rng, net) if ev else np.array([])
    kinds = np.array([e[2] for e in ev])
    keep = obs >= 0
    obs, kinds = obs[keep], kinds[keep]
    is_click = kinds != "break"
    clicks = pd.DataFrame({"session_id": session_id,
                           "t_ns": (obs[is_click] * 1e6).astype("int64"),
                           "source": kinds[is_click], "label": label})
    b = pd.DataFrame({"session_id": session_id, "t_ns": (obs[~is_click] * 1e6).astype("int64"),
                      "label": label})
    return clicks, b


def generate_humans(n_participants: int, rng: np.random.Generator, net: NetParams,
                    hyper: dict | None = None, combat_sessions: int = 3, combat_s: float = 180.0,
                    mining_sessions: int = 1, mining_s: float = 90.0, prefix: str = "SH"):
    """Synthetic human participants. Returns (clicks, breaks, sessions, params) frames."""
    hyper = hyper or PLACEHOLDER_HYPER
    cl, br, ss, pr = [], [], [], []
    for i in range(n_participants):
        pid = f"{prefix}{i:03d}"
        p = sample_params(rng, hyper)
        pr.append({"participant": pid, **asdict(p)})
        for j in range(combat_sessions):
            sid = f"{pid}-c{j}"
            c, b = to_recorder_frames(sid, "human_combat", human_click_times(p, combat_s, rng), rng, net)
            cl.append(c); br.append(b)
            ss.append({"session_id": sid, "participant": pid, "label": "human_combat",
                       "duration_s": int(combat_s), "events_dropped": 0, "level": "human"})
        for j in range(mining_sessions):
            sid = f"{pid}-m{j}"
            starts, swings, brk = mining_events(mining_s, rng)
            c, b = to_recorder_frames(sid, "human_mining", swings, rng, net, starts, brk)
            cl.append(c); br.append(b)
            ss.append({"session_id": sid, "participant": pid, "label": "human_mining",
                       "duration_s": int(mining_s), "events_dropped": 0, "level": "human"})
    return _frames(cl, br, ss), pd.DataFrame(pr)


def _frames(cl, br, ss):
    clicks = pd.concat(cl, ignore_index=True)
    breaks = pd.concat([b for b in br if len(b)], ignore_index=True) if any(len(b) for b in br) \
        else pd.DataFrame(columns=["session_id", "t_ns", "label"])
    sessions = pd.DataFrame(ss)
    sessions["start_utc"] = ""
    sessions["end_utc"] = ""
    sessions["activity"] = sessions["label"].map(C.ACTIVITY)
    sessions["origin"] = "synthetic"
    return clicks, breaks, sessions

```

`ml/src/synthetic.py`:

```py
"""Phase B orchestration: build the synthetic dataset (humans + standard adversary levels).

Every synthetic row carries origin = "synthetic"; files are written as
data/processed/synthetic_*.csv. The optimized adversary is generated later (adversarial.py)
because it needs a trained detector.

    python -m src.synthetic
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as C
from .adversary import generate_attackers
from .data import Dataset, build_click_streams, load_raw
from .observation import NetParams, calibrate_jitter
from .synth_humans import PLACEHOLDER_HYPER, fit_hyper, generate_humans

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

N_HUMANS = 40
N_ATTACKERS = 24            # per adversary level
STANDARD_LEVELS = ["none", "uniform", "gaussian", "human_like"]


def calibration_from_real() -> tuple[NetParams, dict, dict]:
    """Jitter and human hyper-parameters from real data when possible (else placeholders)."""
    real = load_raw()
    streams = build_click_streams(real) if len(real.clicks) else pd.DataFrame(
        columns=["iv_ms", "held", "label", "participant"])
    net = calibrate_jitter(streams["iv_ms"].dropna().to_numpy()) if len(streams) else NetParams()
    hyper, info = fit_hyper(streams) if len(streams) else (None, {"active_intervals": 0})
    info["mode"] = "fitted" if hyper else "placeholder"
    info["jitter_sigma_ms"] = net.sigma_ms
    info["jitter_source"] = net.source
    return net, (hyper or PLACEHOLDER_HYPER), info


def concat_ds(parts: list[tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]]) -> Dataset:
    cl = pd.concat([p[0] for p in parts], ignore_index=True)
    nonempty = [p[1] for p in parts if len(p[1])]
    br = pd.concat(nonempty, ignore_index=True) if nonempty else         pd.DataFrame({"session_id": pd.Series(dtype=str), "t_ns": pd.Series(dtype="int64"),
                      "label": pd.Series(dtype=str)})
    ss = pd.concat([p[2] for p in parts], ignore_index=True)
    ss["origin"] = "synthetic"
    for df in (cl, br):
        df["origin"] = "synthetic"
        df["participant"] = df["session_id"].map(ss.set_index("session_id")["participant"])
    return Dataset(cl, pd.DataFrame(), br, ss, [])


def build(seed: int = C.SEED, n_humans: int = N_HUMANS, n_attackers: int = N_ATTACKERS,
          levels=STANDARD_LEVELS, net: NetParams | None = None, hyper: dict | None = None):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if net is None or hyper is None:
        net, hyper, _ = calibration_from_real()
    parts, params = [], []
    (h, hp) = generate_humans(n_humans, rng, net, hyper)
    parts.append(h); params.append(hp.assign(level="human"))
    for lvl in levels:
        a, ap = generate_attackers(lvl, n_attackers, rng, net, hyper)
        parts.append(a); params.append(ap)
    ds = concat_ds(parts)
    return ds, pd.concat(params, ignore_index=True)


def plot_overview(streams: pd.DataFrame, real_streams: pd.DataFrame | None, path) -> None:
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    groups = [("human", "synthetic human (combat)")] + [(l, f"adversary: {l}") for l in STANDARD_LEVELS]
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, len(groups) + (1 if real_streams is not None else 0),
                             figsize=(3.0 * (len(groups) + 1), 3.0), sharey=False)
    bins = np.arange(0, 400, 5)
    for ax, (lvl, title) in zip(axes, groups):
        s = streams[(streams["level"] == lvl) & ~streams["held"].astype(bool)]
        if lvl == "human":
            s = s[s["label"] == "human_combat"]
        ax.hist(s["iv_ms"].dropna(), bins=bins, color="#888" if lvl == "human" else "#c0504d")
        ax.set_title(title, fontsize=9)
        ax.set_xlabel("interval (ms)")
    if real_streams is not None:
        r = real_streams[~real_streams["held"].astype(bool)]["iv_ms"].dropna()
        axes[-1].hist(r, bins=bins, color="#3b6ea5")
        axes[-1].set_title(f"REAL human, non-held (n={len(r)})", fontsize=9)
        axes[-1].set_xlabel("interval (ms)")
    fig.suptitle(f"Observed click intervals after tick quantization - SYNTHETIC panels are {C.DEMO_TAG}",
                 fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=120)
    plt.close(fig)


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s %(message)s")
    net, hyper, info = calibration_from_real()
    log.info("calibration: %s", {k: v for k, v in info.items() if k != "fitted_per_participant"})
    ds, params = build(net=net, hyper=hyper)
    streams = build_click_streams(ds)
    ds.clicks.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "synthetic_clicks.csv", index=False)
    ds.breaks.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "synthetic_breaks.csv", index=False)
    ds.sessions.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "synthetic_sessions.csv", index=False)
    streams.to_csv(C.PROCESSED_DIR / "synthetic_click_streams.csv", index=False)
    params.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "synthetic_generator_params.csv", index=False)
    (C.TAB_DIR / "synthetic_calibration.json").write_text(json.dumps(
        {"human_generator": info["mode"], "hyper": hyper, "info": info}, indent=2, default=float))
    real = load_raw()
    real_streams = build_click_streams(real) if len(real.clicks) else None
    plot_overview(streams, real_streams, C.FIG_DIR / "synthetic_interval_overview.png")
    summ = streams.groupby(["level", "label"]).agg(
        sessions=("session_id", "nunique"), participants=("participant", "nunique"),
        accepted_swings=("t_ms", "size"), held=("held", "sum"), duplicates=("n_dup", "sum"),
        median_iv_ms=("iv_ms", "median")).reset_index()
    summ.insert(0, "origin", "synthetic")
    summ.to_csv(C.TAB_DIR / "synthetic_summary.csv", index=False)
    log.info("\n%s", summ.to_string(index=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`ml/src/train.py`:

```py
"""Phase D - model definitions.

Every detector exposes the same interface:
    det = make_detector(name); det.fit(train_windows); det.score(windows) -> np.ndarray
Higher score = more suspicious. Thresholds are NOT set here (see evaluate.calibrate).

Detectors
  baseline_orig_raw   ORIGINAL thesis rule on raw LEFT_CLICK_AIR/BLOCK events, no de-dup (fixed rule)
  baseline_orig_clean ORIGINAL rule (0 < diff < 66 ms) on cleaned active intervals (fixed rule)
  cv_rule             one explainable statistic: score = -cv (calibrated threshold)
  iforest, ocsvm      unsupervised, trained on HUMAN windows only
  rf, xgb             supervised
  cnn1d               optional 1D-CNN on raw interval sequences (needs torch)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest, RandomForestClassifier
from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.svm import OneClassSVM

from . import config as C
from .features import FEATURES

FIXED_RULES = {"baseline_orig_raw", "baseline_orig_clean"}


def is_attack(df: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
    return (~df["label"].isin(C.HUMAN_LABELS)).to_numpy().astype(int)


def X_of(df: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
    return df[FEATURES].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)


class FixedRule:
    """Original thesis rule at window level: number of intervals violating 0 < diff < 66 ms.

    A window is flagged when the count is >= 1 (the plugin raises VL on every violating click).
    """
    def __init__(self, column: str):
        self.column = column
    def fit(self, df):
        return self
    def score(self, df):
        return df[self.column].to_numpy(dtype=float)


class CvRule:
    def fit(self, df):
        return self
    def score(self, df):
        return -df["cv"].to_numpy(dtype=float)


class Unsupervised:
    def __init__(self, kind: str, seed: int):
        if kind == "iforest":
            self.m = IsolationForest(n_estimators=300, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)
        else:
            self.m = make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), OneClassSVM(nu=0.05, gamma="scale"))
        self.kind = kind
    def fit(self, df):
        h = df[is_attack(df) == 0]
        X = X_of(h)
        if self.kind == "ocsvm" and len(X) > 5000:   # O(n^2) kernel: subsample deterministically
            X = X[np.random.default_rng(C.SEED).choice(len(X), 5000, replace=False)]
        self.m.fit(X)
        return self
    def score(self, df):
        return -self.m.decision_function(X_of(df))


class Supervised:
    def __init__(self, kind: str, seed: int):
        self.kind = kind
        if kind == "rf":
            self.m = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=300, min_samples_leaf=2, n_jobs=-1,
                                            random_state=seed)
        else:
            from xgboost import XGBClassifier
            self.m = XGBClassifier(n_estimators=300, max_depth=4, learning_rate=0.1, subsample=0.9,
                                   colsample_bytree=0.9, tree_method="hist", random_state=seed,
                                   n_jobs=4, eval_metric="logloss")
    def fit(self, df):
        self.m.fit(X_of(df), is_attack(df))
        return self
    def score(self, df):
        return self.m.predict_proba(X_of(df))[:, 1]


def torch_available() -> bool:
    try:
        import torch  # noqa: F401
        return True
    except ImportError:
        return False


class CNN1D:
    """Small 1D-CNN over the window's active intervals (log-scaled, padded to 49)."""
    L = C.WINDOW_CLICKS - 1

    def __init__(self, seed: int, epochs: int = 8):
        self.seed, self.epochs = seed, epochs

    def _tensor(self, df):
        import torch
        out = np.zeros((len(df), 1, self.L), np.float32)
        for i, s in enumerate(df["seq"]):
            s = np.log(np.clip(np.asarray(s, np.float32), 1, 2000)) / np.log(2000)
            out[i, 0, :min(len(s), self.L)] = s[:self.L]
        return torch.from_numpy(out)

    def fit(self, df):
        import torch
        from torch import nn
        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv1d(1, 16, 5, padding=2), nn.ReLU(), nn.Conv1d(16, 32, 5, padding=2), nn.ReLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveMaxPool1d(1), nn.Flatten(), nn.Linear(32, 1))
        X, y = self._tensor(df), torch.tensor(is_attack(df), dtype=torch.float32)
        opt = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=3e-3)
        lossf = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()
        g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(self.seed)
        for _ in range(self.epochs):
            perm = torch.randperm(len(X), generator=g)
            for i in range(0, len(X), 256):
                idx = perm[i:i + 256]
                opt.zero_grad()
                loss = lossf(self.net(X[idx]).squeeze(1), y[idx])
                loss.backward()
                opt.step()
        return self

    def score(self, df):
        import torch
        with torch.no_grad():
            return torch.sigmoid(self.net(self._tensor(df)).squeeze(1)).numpy()


def make_detector(name: str, seed: int = C.SEED):
    if name == "baseline_orig_raw":
        return FixedRule("naive_java_violations")
    if name == "baseline_orig_clean":
        return FixedRule("clean_violations")
    if name == "cv_rule":
        return CvRule()
    if name in ("iforest", "ocsvm"):
        return Unsupervised(name, seed)
    if name in ("rf", "xgb"):
        return Supervised(name, seed)
    if name == "cnn1d":
        return CNN1D(seed)
    raise ValueError(name)


def available_models(include_cnn: bool = True) -> list[str]:
    m = ["baseline_orig_raw", "baseline_orig_clean", "cv_rule", "iforest", "ocsvm", "rf", "xgb"]
    if include_cnn and torch_available():
        m.append("cnn1d")
    return m

```

`ml/tests/test_data.py`:

```py
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from src import config as C
from src.data import (SchemaError, _coerce, clean_click_stream, naive_intervals, normalise_label,
                      quantization_summary, tick_residuals, trim_moves)


def clicks(events):
    """events: list of (t_ms, source)."""
    return pd.DataFrame({"t_ns": [int(t * 1e6) for t, _ in events],
                         "source": [s for _, s in events]})


def breaks(ts):
    return pd.DataFrame({"t_ns": [int(t * 1e6) for t in ts]})


def test_legacy_label_normalised():
    assert normalise_label("human") == "human_unspecified"
    assert normalise_label("auto_fixed") == "auto_fixed"


def test_one_physical_click_three_events_is_one_click():
    # interact_block + interact_air + swing within 5 ms, twice, 200 ms apart
    ev = [(0, "interact_block"), (2, "interact_air"), (4, "swing"),
          (200, "interact_air"), (203, "swing"), (205, "swing")]
    s = clean_click_stream(clicks(ev))
    assert len(s) == 2
    assert s["n_dup"].tolist() == [0, 1]
    assert s["iv_ms"].iloc[1] == pytest.approx(199)


def test_dedup_is_relative_to_last_accepted_swing():
    s = clean_click_stream(clicks([(0, "swing"), (10, "swing"), (20, "swing"), (31, "swing")]))
    # 10 merged into 0; 20 accepted (20 >= 15); 31 merged into 20
    assert s["t_ms"].tolist() == [0, 20]
    assert s["n_dup"].tolist() == [1, 1]


def test_held_button_mining_run():
    # dig start, swings every tick for 6 ticks, break, next block continues held
    ev = [(1000, "interact_block"), (1002, "swing")]
    ev += [(1002 + 50 * k, "swing") for k in range(1, 7)]
    ev += [(1305, "interact_block"), (1352, "swing"), (1402, "swing")]
    s = clean_click_stream(clicks(ev), breaks([1303]))
    held = s["held"].tolist()
    assert held[0] is False or held[0] == False        # initial press is a real click
    assert all(held[1:7])                              # held swings during the dig
    assert all(held[7:])                               # continues across a broken block
    # release then click in air 400 ms later -> active again
    ev2 = ev + [(1802, "swing"), (1850, "swing")]
    s2 = clean_click_stream(clicks(ev2), breaks([1303]))
    assert s2["held"].tolist()[-2:] == [False, False]


def test_fast_clicking_in_air_is_never_held():
    ev = [(50 * k, "swing") for k in range(20)]
    s = clean_click_stream(clicks(ev))
    assert not s["held"].any()


def test_naive_interact_intervals_floor_ms():
    ev = clicks([(0.0, "interact_block"), (3.7, "interact_air"), (70.2, "swing"), (90.9, "interact_air")])
    iv = naive_intervals(ev.assign(session_id="s"), {"interact_air", "interact_block"})["iv_ms"]
    assert iv.tolist() == [3.0, 87.0]


def test_tick_residuals_and_summary():
    r = tick_residuals(np.array([48, 52, 99, 151, 175]))
    assert np.allclose(r, [-2, 2, -1, 1, -25]) or np.allclose(r, [-2, 2, -1, 1, 25])
    q = quantization_summary(np.array([50, 100, 150, 49, 101] * 10, dtype=float))
    assert q["frac_within_5ms"] == 1.0


def test_trim_moves_relative_to_first_move():
    m = pd.DataFrame({"session_id": "s", "t_ns": [int(10e9), int(14.9e9), int(15e9), int(16e9)]})
    assert len(trim_moves(m)) == 2


def test_instant_break_then_held_swings():
    # pattern observed in the real data: interact_block, break 0.2 ms later, then the click's
    # swing, then one swing per tick while the button stays held
    ev = [(0, "interact_block"), (0.6, "swing"), (50.1, "swing"), (105.4, "swing"),
          (657.0, "interact_block"), (657.6, "swing"), (705.0, "swing")]
    s = clean_click_stream(clicks(ev), breaks([0.2, 657.2]))
    assert s["held"].tolist() == [False, True, True, False, True]


def test_schema_rejects_unknown_label(tmp_path):
    df = pd.DataFrame({"session_id": ["a"], "t_ns": ["1"], "source": ["swing"], "label": ["cheater"]})
    with pytest.raises(SchemaError):
        _coerce(df, "clicks", tmp_path / "clicks.csv")


def test_original_threshold_is_java_integer_division():
    assert C.ORIG_CLICK_MIN_DIFF_MS == 66
```

`ml/tests/test_features.py`:

```py
import numpy as np
import pytest
from scipy import stats

from src import config as C
from src.features import (interval_stats, max_clicks_in_1s, percentile_linear, window_features)


def test_percentile_matches_numpy():
    a = np.sort(np.array([3, 1, 4, 1, 5, 9, 2, 6, 5, 3], dtype=float))
    for q in (0.05, 0.5, 0.95):
        assert percentile_linear(a, q) == pytest.approx(np.percentile(a, q * 100))


def test_moments_match_scipy_biased():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    A = rng.lognormal(np.log(120), 0.3, 200)
    s = interval_stats(A)
    assert s["mean"] == pytest.approx(A.mean())
    assert s["std"] == pytest.approx(A.std(ddof=0))
    assert s["skew"] == pytest.approx(stats.skew(A, bias=True))
    assert s["kurt"] == pytest.approx(stats.kurtosis(A, fisher=True, bias=True))


def test_constant_intervals():
    s = interval_stats(np.full(20, 50.0))
    assert s["std"] == 0 and s["cv"] == 0 and s["skew"] == 0 and s["kurt"] == 0
    assert s["entropy"] == 0           # one histogram bin
    assert s["ac1"] == 0
    assert s["tick_frac"] == 1.0


def test_alternating_intervals_autocorrelation():
    s = interval_stats(np.array([50.0, 100.0] * 10))
    assert s["ac1"] < -0.9
    assert s["entropy"] == pytest.approx(1.0)  # two equally likely bins -> 1 bit


def test_histogram_overflow_bin():
    s = interval_stats(np.array([600.0, 700.0, 10.0, 12.0]))
    assert s["entropy"] == pytest.approx(1.0)  # bins {0, overflow}


def test_max_cps():
    assert max_clicks_in_1s(np.arange(0, 3000, 100.0)) == 10
    assert max_clicks_in_1s(np.array([0, 999.0])) == 2
    assert max_clicks_in_1s(np.array([0, 1000.0])) == 1


def test_window_features_held_and_pause_handling():
    t = np.array([0, 100, 200, 250, 300, 350, 2000] + [2000 + 100 * k for k in range(1, 12)], float)
    held = np.zeros(len(t), bool); held[3:6] = True       # 250, 300, 350 are held swings
    f = window_features(t, held, np.zeros(len(t)))
    # kept intervals: 100,100,(held x3 dropped),1650(pause),100 x 11
    assert f["n_active"] == 13
    assert f["pause_frac"] == pytest.approx(1 / 14)
    assert f["mean"] == pytest.approx(100.0)
    assert f["held_frac"] == pytest.approx(3 / 18)
    assert f["valid"] and f["routed"] == "click"


def test_window_routed_to_mining_and_invalid():
    t = np.arange(0, 50 * 30, 50.0)
    held = np.ones(len(t), bool); held[0] = False
    f = window_features(t, held, np.zeros(len(t)))
    assert f["routed"] == "mining" and not f["valid"]


def test_dup_frac():
    f = window_features(np.arange(0, 1500, 100.0), np.zeros(15, bool), np.r_[np.ones(5), np.zeros(10)])
    assert f["dup_frac"] == pytest.approx(5 / 20)


def test_calibrate_threshold_respects_target():
    from src.evaluate import calibrate
    s = np.arange(1, 1001, dtype=float)
    thr = calibrate(s, 0.01)
    assert np.mean(s > thr) <= 0.01 and np.mean(s > thr) == pytest.approx(0.01)
    assert calibrate(s[:500], 0.001) == 500.0  # resolution limit: max of calibration set
```

`ml/tests/test_synthetic.py`:

```py
import numpy as np

from src.adversary import AttackParams, attack_click_times, effective_cps, sample_attack
from src.data import tick_residuals
from src.observation import NetParams, enforce_order, lag_spikes, observe
from src.synth_humans import human_click_times, sample_params


def test_observation_quantizes_to_client_ticks_without_jitter():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    t = np.cumsum(rng.uniform(60, 300, 200))
    s = observe(t, rng, NetParams(sigma_ms=0.0))
    iv = np.diff(s)
    iv = iv[iv > 1]  # same-tick back-to-back sends excluded
    assert np.all(np.abs(tick_residuals(iv)) < 1.0)


def test_observation_keeps_order():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2)
    s = observe(np.sort(rng.uniform(0, 5000, 300)), rng, NetParams(sigma_ms=10.0))
    assert np.all(np.diff(s) > 0)


def test_fixed_autoclicker_is_strictly_periodic_before_observation():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    t = attack_click_times(AttackParams("none", 80.0, bout_s=(100, 100), gap_s=(1, 1)), 10, rng)
    assert np.allclose(np.diff(t), 80.0)


def test_effective_cps_matches_fixed_rate():
    assert abs(effective_cps({"median_ms": 100.0, "spread": 0.0, "rho": 0.0, "pause_prob": 0.0,
                              "pause_median_ms": 400.0}, np.random.default_rng(0), 500) - 10.0) < 1e-6


def test_human_generator_rate_in_placeholder_range():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(4)
    p = sample_params(rng)
    t = human_click_times(p, 120, rng)
    iv = np.diff(t)
    assert np.all(iv >= 35.0)
    assert 2 < len(t) / 120 < 15


def test_lag_spikes_bunch_packets():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(5)
    s = np.arange(0, 20000, 100.0)
    out = lag_spikes(s, rng, rate_per_s=0.5, dur_ms=(300, 300))
    assert np.all(np.diff(out) > 0)
    assert np.sum(np.diff(out) < 1.0) > 0  # some packets released together


def test_enforce_order():
    assert np.all(np.diff(enforce_order(np.array([0, 5, 3, 3, 10.0]))) > 0)


def test_sample_levels():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(6)
    for lvl in ("none", "uniform", "gaussian", "human_like"):
        assert sample_attack(lvl, rng).median_ms > 0
```

`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/ClickFeatureExtractor.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import java.util.Arrays;

/**
 * Caracteristicas de una ventana de clics (port exacto de ml/src/features.py window_features).
 * Codigo puro (sin Bukkit): se ejecuta en el hilo de ML, nunca en el hilo principal.
 */
public final class ClickFeatureExtractor {

    /** Orden de entrada del modelo ONNX (debe coincidir con click_model.json). */
    public static final String[] FEATURES = {"mean", "std", "cv", "skew", "kurt", "min", "p05", "p95",
            "entropy", "ac1", "max_cps_1s", "tick_frac", "dup_frac", "pause_frac"};

    public static final double PAUSE_MS = 1000.0;
    public static final int MIN_INTERVALS = 10;
    public static final double HELD_ROUTE_FRAC = 0.5;
    public static final double TICK_MS = 50.0;
    public static final double TICK_NEAR_MS = 5.0;
    private static final double HIST_BIN_MS = 25.0;
    private static final int HIST_BINS = 21; // 20 bins de 25 ms en [0, 500) + desbordamiento

    private ClickFeatureExtractor() { }

    public static final class Result {
        public final double[] values = new double[FEATURES.length];
        public boolean valid;
        public boolean routedMining;
        public double heldFrac;
        public int nActive;
        public int nSwings;

        public float[] asFloat() {
            float[] x = new float[values.length];
            for (int i = 0; i < x.length; i++) x[i] = (float) values[i];
            return x;
        }

        public double get(String name) {
            for (int i = 0; i < FEATURES.length; i++) if (FEATURES[i].equals(name)) return values[i];
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(name);
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param t     instantes de los swings aceptados (ms), ordenados
     * @param held  swing generado por boton mantenido
     * @param nDup  duplicados absorbidos por cada swing
     */
    public static Result compute(double[] t, boolean[] held, int[] nDup) {
        Result r = new Result();
        int n = t.length;
        r.nSwings = n;
        Arrays.fill(r.values, Double.NaN);
        if (n == 0) return r;

        int nHeld = 0;
        long dupSum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (held[i]) nHeld++;
            dupSum += nDup[i];
        }
        r.heldFrac = (double) nHeld / n;
        r.routedMining = r.heldFrac >= HELD_ROUTE_FRAC;

        // intervalos conservados: el swing i no es "held"; pausas (> 1 s) fuera de las estadisticas
        double[] a = new double[n];
        int kept = 0, pauses = 0, na = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            if (held[i]) continue;
            double d = t[i] - t[i - 1];
            kept++;
            if (d > PAUSE_MS) pauses++;
            else a[na++] = d;
        }
        a = Arrays.copyOf(a, na);
        r.nActive = na;
        r.valid = na >= MIN_INTERVALS;
        set(r, "dup_frac", (double) dupSum / (n + dupSum));
        set(r, "pause_frac", kept > 0 ? (double) pauses / kept : Double.NaN);
        if (!r.valid) return r;

        double mean = 0;
        for (double v : a) mean += v;
        mean /= na;
        double m2 = 0, m3 = 0, m4 = 0;
        for (double v : a) {
            double d = v - mean, d2 = d * d;
            m2 += d2;
            m3 += d2 * d;
            m4 += d2 * d2;
        }
        double den = m2;            // suma de cuadrados (para la autocorrelacion)
        m2 /= na; m3 /= na; m4 /= na;
        double std = Math.sqrt(m2);
        set(r, "mean", mean);
        set(r, "std", std);
        set(r, "cv", mean > 0 ? std / mean : 0.0);
        set(r, "skew", m2 > 1e-12 ? m3 / Math.pow(m2, 1.5) : 0.0);
        set(r, "kurt", m2 > 1e-12 ? m4 / (m2 * m2) - 3.0 : 0.0);

        double[] s = a.clone();
        Arrays.sort(s);
        set(r, "min", s[0]);
        set(r, "p05", percentileLinear(s, 0.05));
        set(r, "p95", percentileLinear(s, 0.95));

        int[] counts = new int[HIST_BINS];
        for (double v : a) counts[Math.min((int) Math.floor(v / HIST_BIN_MS), HIST_BINS - 1)]++;
        double h = 0;
        for (int c : counts) {
            if (c == 0) continue;
            double p = (double) c / na;
            h -= p * (Math.log(p) / Math.log(2));
        }
        set(r, "entropy", h);

        double num = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < na - 1; i++) num += (a[i] - mean) * (a[i + 1] - mean);
        set(r, "ac1", den > 1e-12 && na > 1 ? num / den : 0.0);

        int near = 0;
        for (double v : a) {
            double res = v - TICK_MS * Math.rint(v / TICK_MS); // rint = redondeo al par (como numpy)
            if (Math.abs(res) <= TICK_NEAR_MS) near++;
        }
        set(r, "tick_frac", (double) near / na);
        set(r, "max_cps_1s", maxClicksIn1s(t, held));
        return r;
    }

    static double percentileLinear(double[] sorted, double q) {
        int n = sorted.length;
        double pos = q * (n - 1);
        int lo = (int) Math.floor(pos);
        int hi = Math.min(lo + 1, n - 1);
        return sorted[lo] + (pos - lo) * (sorted[hi] - sorted[lo]);
    }

    static int maxClicksIn1s(double[] t, boolean[] held) {
        double[] act = new double[t.length];
        int m = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < t.length; i++) if (!held[i]) act[m++] = t[i];
        int best = 0, j = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            while (act[i] - act[j] >= 1000.0) j++;
            best = Math.max(best, i - j + 1);
        }
        return best;
    }

    private static void set(Result r, String name, double v) {
        for (int i = 0; i < FEATURES.length; i++) {
            if (FEATURES[i].equals(name)) {
                r.values[i] = v;
                return;
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException(name);
    }
}

```

`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/ClickModel.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import ai.onnxruntime.OnnxTensor;
import ai.onnxruntime.OnnxValue;
import ai.onnxruntime.OrtEnvironment;
import ai.onnxruntime.OrtException;
import ai.onnxruntime.OrtSession;
import com.google.gson.JsonArray;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Envoltorio del modelo ONNX (unica clase que depende de ONNX Runtime). Si la libreria no esta en el
 * classpath, cargar esta clase lanza NoClassDefFoundError y el plugin vuelve al detector por umbral.
 */
public final class ClickModel implements AutoCloseable {

    private final OrtEnvironment env;
    private final OrtSession session;
    private final String inputName;
    public final double threshold;
    public final boolean demo;
    public final String description;

    private ClickModel(OrtEnvironment env, OrtSession session, double threshold, boolean demo, String description) {
        this.env = env;
        this.session = session;
        this.inputName = session.getInputNames().iterator().next();
        this.threshold = threshold;
        this.demo = demo;
        this.description = description;
    }

    /** Carga el modelo y valida que su lista de features coincide con ClickFeatureExtractor. */
    public static ClickModel load(Path onnx, Path metadata) throws IOException, OrtException {
        JsonObject meta = JsonParser.parseString(Files.readString(metadata, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).getAsJsonObject();
        JsonArray feats = meta.getAsJsonArray("features");
        if (feats.size() != ClickFeatureExtractor.FEATURES.length) {
            throw new IOException("click_model.json: numero de features distinto al del extractor Java");
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < feats.size(); i++) {
            if (!feats.get(i).getAsString().equals(ClickFeatureExtractor.FEATURES[i])) {
                throw new IOException("click_model.json: feature " + i + " = " + feats.get(i).getAsString()
                        + ", se esperaba " + ClickFeatureExtractor.FEATURES[i]);
            }
        }
        OrtEnvironment env = OrtEnvironment.getEnvironment();
        OrtSession.SessionOptions opts = new OrtSession.SessionOptions();
        opts.setIntraOpNumThreads(1); // impacto minimo en el servidor
        OrtSession session = env.createSession(onnx.toString(), opts);
        double thr = meta.get("threshold_fpr_1pct").getAsDouble();
        boolean demo = meta.has("demo") && meta.get("demo").getAsBoolean();
        String desc = meta.get("model").getAsString() + (demo ? " (DEMO, entrenado con datos sinteticos)" : "");
        return new ClickModel(env, session, thr, demo, desc);
    }

    /** Probabilidad de automatizacion para un vector de features (orden FEATURES). */
    public float score(float[] x) throws OrtException {
        try (OnnxTensor in = OnnxTensor.createTensor(env, new float[][]{x});
             OrtSession.Result out = session.run(Collections.singletonMap(inputName, in))) {
            for (Map.Entry<String, OnnxValue> e : out) {
                Object v = e.getValue().getValue();
                if (v instanceof float[][]) {
                    float[][] p = (float[][]) v;
                    return p[0][p[0].length - 1];
                }
            }
            throw new OrtException("El modelo no devolvio probabilidades");
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void close() throws OrtException {
        session.close();
    }
}

```

`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/ClickStreamCleaner.java`:

```java
package org.example;

/**
 * Limpieza causal del flujo de clics de UN jugador (mismo algoritmo que ml/src/data.py
 * clean_click_stream; los tests comparan ambas implementaciones con fixtures).
 *
 * 1. Solo el swing (PlayerAnimationEvent ARM_SWING) es un clic; los PlayerInteractEvent
 *    LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK y las roturas de bloque solo marcan el inicio de una excavacion.
 * 2. Un swing a menos de DEDUP_MS del ultimo swing aceptado es un duplicado (se cuenta y se descarta).
 * 3. Boton mantenido (minar): una marca de excavacion abre un "dig"; mientras el boton sigue pulsado el
 *    cliente envia un swing por tick, asi que los swings separados <= HELD_MAX_IV_MS se marcan como
 *    "held" (generados por el juego, no por el jugador) y no se evaluan como clics.
 *
 * No es thread-safe: se usa una instancia por jugador desde el hilo principal.
 */
public final class ClickStreamCleaner {

    public static final double DEDUP_MS = 15.0;
    public static final double HELD_MAX_IV_MS = 65.0;
    public static final double FRESH_DIG_MS = 15.0;

    /** Resultado de procesar un swing. */
    public static final class Swing {
        public final double tMs;
        /** Intervalo con el swing aceptado anterior (NaN para el primero). */
        public final double ivMs;
        public final boolean held;
        /** Duplicados absorbidos por este swing (se incrementa despues de crearlo). */
        public int nDup;

        Swing(double tMs, double ivMs, boolean held) {
            this.tMs = tMs;
            this.ivMs = ivMs;
            this.held = held;
        }
    }

    private boolean digging = false;
    private boolean prevHeld = false;
    private double lastDig = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
    private Swing last = null;

    /** LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK o rotura de bloque. */
    public void onDigMarker(double tMs) {
        digging = true;
        lastDig = tMs;
    }

    /**
     * Procesa un swing.
     * @return el swing aceptado, o null si es un duplicado (absorbido por el ultimo aceptado)
     */
    public Swing onSwing(double tMs) {
        if (last != null && tMs - last.tMs < DEDUP_MS) {
            last.nDup++;
            return null;
        }
        double iv = last == null ? Double.NaN : tMs - last.tMs;
        boolean fresh = (tMs - lastDig) <= FRESH_DIG_MS;
        boolean held = digging && !Double.isNaN(iv) && iv <= HELD_MAX_IV_MS && (!fresh || prevHeld);
        if (!held && !fresh) {
            digging = false;
        }
        prevHeld = held;
        last = new Swing(tMs, iv, held);
        return last;
    }
}

```

`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/ComandosCiberPlugin.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.command.Command;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandExecutor;
import org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

public class ComandosCiberPlugin implements CommandExecutor {

    private final Proyecto_ciberPlugin plugin;

    public ComandosCiberPlugin(Proyecto_ciberPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {

        if (args.length > 0 && args[0].equalsIgnoreCase("reload")) {
            plugin.reloadPluginConfig();

            // BASTIONADO 6: Auditoria de Administradores (Insider Threat)
            // Registra en la consola del servidor quien hizo el cambio
            plugin.getLogger().warning("AUDITORIA SEGURIDAD: El administrador [" + sender.getName() + "] ha recargado la configuracion del Anti-Cheat.");

            sender.sendMessage("§8[§a✔§8] §aConfiguracion de seguridad recargada en caliente.");
            return true;
        }

        // NUEVO: grabacion de sesiones para el dataset
        if (args.length > 0 && args[0].equalsIgnoreCase("record")) {
            return handleRecord(sender, args);
        }

        sender.sendMessage("§8[§c!§8] §cUso incorrecto. Usa: /ciberplugin <reload|record>");
        return true;
    }

    // NUEVO
    private boolean handleRecord(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {
        // BASTIONADO: comprobacion explicita de permisos (defensa en profundidad, ademas del plugin.yml)
        if (!sender.hasPermission("ciberplugin.admin")) {
            sender.sendMessage("§cNo tienes permisos de administrador para usar este comando.");
            return true;
        }

        SessionRecorder rec = plugin.getRecorder();

        // /ciberplugin record start <jugador> <etiqueta> <participante>
        if (args.length >= 5 && args[1].equalsIgnoreCase("start")) {
            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[2]);
            if (target == null) {
                sender.sendMessage("§cJugador no conectado.");
                return true;
            }
            if (rec.isRecording(target.getUniqueId())) {
                sender.sendMessage("§cYa hay una sesión activa para ese jugador.");
                return true;
            }
            SessionRecorder.Session s = rec.startSession(target, args[3], args[4]);
            if (s == null) {
                sender.sendMessage("§cEtiqueta o participante no válidos (solo letras, números y _).");
                return true;
            }
            plugin.getLogger().warning("AUDITORIA SEGURIDAD: [" + sender.getName()
                    + "] inicio grabacion " + s.sessionId + " (" + s.label + ", " + s.participant + ")");
            sender.sendMessage("§aGrabando sesión §e" + s.sessionId + " §a(" + s.label + ", " + s.participant + ")");
            return true;
        }

        // /ciberplugin record stop <jugador>
        if (args.length >= 3 && args[1].equalsIgnoreCase("stop")) {
            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[2]);
            SessionRecorder.Session s = (target == null) ? null : rec.stopSession(target.getUniqueId());
            if (s == null) {
                sender.sendMessage("§cNo había sesión activa para ese jugador.");
            } else {
                plugin.getLogger().warning("AUDITORIA SEGURIDAD: [" + sender.getName()
                        + "] detuvo grabacion " + s.sessionId);
                sender.sendMessage("§aSesión §e" + s.sessionId + " §adetenida.");
            }
            return true;
        }

        sender.sendMessage("§8[§c!§8] §cUso: /ciberplugin record start <jugador> <etiqueta> <participante> | stop <jugador>");
        return true;
    }
}
```
