`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/MlClickDetector.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ArrayBlockingQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Detector de AutoClicker por ML con ventanas de 50 swings (paso 25).
 *
 * Hilo principal: solo anade el swing al buffer del jugador y, cada 25 swings, copia una ventana.
 * Hilo "CiberPlugin-ML": calcula las features y ejecuta el modelo. La cola es acotada: si se llena
 * la ventana se descarta y se cuenta (backpressure, igual que SessionRecorder), nunca bloquea el tick.
 */
public final class MlClickDetector {

    public static final int WINDOW = 50;
    public static final int STRIDE = 25;
    private static final int QUEUE_CAPACITY = 256;

    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface Scorer {
        float score(float[] x) throws Exception;
    }

    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface Sink {
        /** Llamado desde el hilo de ML; el receptor debe volver al hilo principal si toca Bukkit. */
        void onWindowScored(UUID player, double score, ClickFeatureExtractor.Result features);
    }

    private final Scorer scorer;
    private final Sink sink;
    private final Map<UUID, List<ClickStreamCleaner.Swing>> buffers = new HashMap<>();
    private final ThreadPoolExecutor executor;
    private final AtomicLong dropped = new AtomicLong();
    private final AtomicLong errors = new AtomicLong();

    public MlClickDetector(Scorer scorer, Sink sink) {
        this.scorer = scorer;
        this.sink = sink;
        this.executor = new ThreadPoolExecutor(1, 1, 0L, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
                new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(QUEUE_CAPACITY),
                r -> {
                    Thread th = new Thread(r, "CiberPlugin-ML");
                    th.setDaemon(true);
                    return th;
                },
                (r, ex) -> dropped.incrementAndGet());
    }

    /** Hilo principal: swing aceptado (no duplicado) del jugador. */
    public void onAccepted(UUID player, ClickStreamCleaner.Swing swing) {
        List<ClickStreamCleaner.Swing> buf = buffers.computeIfAbsent(player, k -> new ArrayList<>(WINDOW + 1));
        buf.add(swing);
        // Se espera al swing 51: asi el contador de duplicados del swing 50 ya es definitivo.
        if (buf.size() < WINDOW + 1) return;
        double[] t = new double[WINDOW];
        boolean[] held = new boolean[WINDOW];
        int[] dup = new int[WINDOW];
        for (int i = 0; i < WINDOW; i++) {
            ClickStreamCleaner.Swing s = buf.get(i);
            t[i] = s.tMs;
            held[i] = s.held;
            dup[i] = s.nDup;
        }
        buf.subList(0, STRIDE).clear();
        executor.execute(() -> score(player, t, held, dup));
    }

    private void score(UUID player, double[] t, boolean[] held, int[] dup) {
        ClickFeatureExtractor.Result f = ClickFeatureExtractor.compute(t, held, dup);
        if (!f.valid || f.routedMining) return; // ventana sin clics suficientes o de minado
        try {
            sink.onWindowScored(player, scorer.score(f.asFloat()), f);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            errors.incrementAndGet();
        }
    }

    public void remove(UUID player) {
        buffers.remove(player);
    }

    public long droppedWindows() {
        return dropped.get();
    }

    public long errors() {
        return errors.get();
    }

    /** Detiene el hilo de ML y espera (max. 2 s) a la inferencia en curso antes de cerrar el modelo. */
    public void shutdown() {
        executor.shutdownNow();
        try {
            executor.awaitTermination(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        buffers.clear();
    }
}

```

`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/Proyecto_ciberPlugin.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.block.Action;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockBreakEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerAnimationEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerAnimationType;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.EquipmentSlot;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public class Proyecto_ciberPlugin extends JavaPlugin implements Listener {

    // MAPAS PARA ESTADOS Y SCORING
    // BASTIONADO: Usamos UUID en lugar de Player para evitar Memory Leaks
    // Limpieza del flujo de clics (swing + de-duplicacion + boton mantenido), una por jugador
    private final Map<UUID, ClickStreamCleaner> clickCleaners = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastBlockBreakTime = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> violationScores = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastAlertTime = new HashMap<>();

    // Umbrales en memoria
    private int maxClicksPerSecond;
    private long minBreakDelay;
    private double maxHorizontalDist;
    private double maxCombatReach;
    private int maxViolations;

    // NUEVO: grabador de sesiones para el dataset
    private SessionRecorder recorder;

    // NUEVO: motor de AutoClicker. threshold = detector original (por defecto); shadow = umbral activo y el
    // modelo ML solo se registra (sin VL); ml = el modelo ML suma VL y el umbral queda como respaldo.
    private volatile String clickEngine = "threshold";   // leido tambien desde el hilo de ML
    private double mlThresholdOverride;
    private MlClickDetector mlDetector;   // null si el motor ML no esta activo o no pudo cargarse
    private volatile ClickModel clickModel;
    private volatile double mlThreshold;

    // BASTIONADO: seudonimizacion con clave (HMAC-SHA256) para auditoria y telemetria
    private volatile Pseudonymizer pseudonymizer;

    // NUEVO
    public SessionRecorder getRecorder() {
        return recorder;
    }

    @Override
    public void onEnable() {
        saveDefaultConfig();
        loadConfigValues();
        loadPseudonymizer();

        recorder = new SessionRecorder(this); // NUEVO
        recorder.open();                      // NUEVO
        initMlEngine();                       // NUEVO

        getServer().getPluginManager().registerEvents(this, this);

        if (getCommand("ciberplugin") != null) {
            getCommand("ciberplugin").setExecutor(new ComandosCiberPlugin(this));
        }

        getLogger().info("CiberPlugin ACTIVADO.");
    }

    private void loadConfigValues() {
        int configClicks = getConfig().getInt("deteccion.autoclicker.max_clicks_per_second", 15);
        long configBreak = getConfig().getLong("deteccion.fastbreak.min_delay_ms", 500);
        double configSpeed = getConfig().getDouble("deteccion.speed.max_horizontal_distance", 1.2);
        double configReach = getConfig().getDouble("deteccion.combat.max_reach", 3.8);
        int configViolations = getConfig().getInt("sanciones.max_violations_before_kick", 10);

        // BASTIONADO: FAIL-SAFE (Validacion de limites)
        this.maxClicksPerSecond = Math.max(5, Math.min(configClicks, 50));
        this.minBreakDelay = Math.max(100L, Math.min(configBreak, 2000L));
        this.maxHorizontalDist = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(configSpeed, 5.0));
        this.maxCombatReach = Math.max(3.0, Math.min(configReach, 6.0));
        this.maxViolations = Math.max(3, Math.min(configViolations, 50));

        if (configClicks != this.maxClicksPerSecond) {
            getLogger().warning("Parametro AutoClicker fuera de limites. Se forzo valor seguro: " + this.maxClicksPerSecond);
        }

        // BASTIONADO: FAIL-SAFE del selector de motor (valor desconocido -> detector por umbral)
        String engine = getConfig().getString("deteccion.autoclicker.engine", "threshold").trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!engine.equals("threshold") && !engine.equals("shadow") && !engine.equals("ml")) {
            getLogger().warning("deteccion.autoclicker.engine invalido (" + engine + "). Se usa 'threshold'.");
            engine = "threshold";
        }
        this.clickEngine = engine;
        double thr = getConfig().getDouble("deteccion.autoclicker.ml_threshold", 0.0);
        // 0 = usar el umbral calibrado del modelo; si se fija a mano se limita a [0.5, 0.9999]
        this.mlThresholdOverride = thr <= 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0.5, Math.min(thr, 0.9999));
    }

    // BASTIONADO: clave HMAC en config.yml (privacidad.hmac_key). Si falta o es invalida se genera una
    // nueva con SecureRandom y se guarda; nunca se escribe en los logs.
    private void loadPseudonymizer() {
        byte[] key = Pseudonymizer.decodeKey(getConfig().getString("privacidad.hmac_key", ""));
        if (key == null) {
            String fresh = Pseudonymizer.generateKey();
            getConfig().set("privacidad.hmac_key", fresh);
            saveConfig();
            key = Pseudonymizer.decodeKey(fresh);
            getLogger().warning("AUDITORIA SEGURIDAD: generada una nueva clave de seudonimizacion (privacidad.hmac_key). "
                    + "Los seudonimos anteriores no se pueden enlazar con los nuevos. No compartas esta clave.");
        }
        this.pseudonymizer = new Pseudonymizer(key);
    }

    // MOTOR ML: carga el modelo ONNX y, ante cualquier fallo, vuelve al detector por umbral (fail-safe)
    private void initMlEngine() {
        shutdownMlEngine();
        if (clickEngine.equals("threshold")) return;
        try {
            File dir = new File(getDataFolder(), "models");
            if (!new File(dir, "click_model.onnx").exists()) saveResource("models/click_model.onnx", false);
            if (!new File(dir, "click_model.json").exists()) saveResource("models/click_model.json", false);
            clickModel = ClickModel.load(new File(dir, "click_model.onnx").toPath(),
                    new File(dir, "click_model.json").toPath());
            mlThreshold = mlThresholdOverride > 0 ? mlThresholdOverride : clickModel.threshold;
            final ClickModel model = clickModel;
            mlDetector = new MlClickDetector(model::score, this::onMlWindowScored);
            getLogger().info("Motor AutoClicker '" + clickEngine + "' con modelo " + model.description
                    + ", umbral " + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", mlThreshold));
            if (model.demo) {
                getLogger().warning("El modelo ML es una DEMO entrenada con datos sinteticos: usar 'shadow' hasta validarlo con datos reales.");
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) { // incluye NoClassDefFoundError si ONNX Runtime no esta disponible
            getLogger().warning("Motor ML no disponible (" + t + "). Se usa el detector por umbral.");
            shutdownMlEngine();
            clickEngine = "threshold";
        }
    }

    private void shutdownMlEngine() {
        if (mlDetector != null) {
            if (mlDetector.droppedWindows() > 0 || mlDetector.errors() > 0) {
                getLogger().warning("Motor ML: ventanas descartadas=" + mlDetector.droppedWindows()
                        + ", errores=" + mlDetector.errors());
            }
            mlDetector.shutdown();
            mlDetector = null;
        }
        if (clickModel != null) {
            try { clickModel.close(); } catch (Exception ignored) { }
            clickModel = null;
        }
    }

    // Llamado desde el hilo de ML: toda accion sobre Bukkit vuelve al hilo principal
    private void onMlWindowScored(UUID uuid, double score, ClickFeatureExtractor.Result f) {
        if (!isEnabled()) return;
        boolean flagged = score > mlThreshold;
        String evidence = String.format(Locale.ROOT,
                "score=%.3f umbral=%.3f | entropia=%.2f bits, media=%.0f ms, cv=%.2f, ac1=%.2f, max_cps=%.0f%s",
                score, mlThreshold, f.get("entropy"), f.get("mean"), f.get("cv"), f.get("ac1"),
                f.get("max_cps_1s"), clickModel != null && clickModel.demo ? " (modelo DEMO)" : "");
        if (clickEngine.equals("shadow")) {
            logMlShadowAsync(uuid, score, flagged, evidence);
            return;
        }
        if (!flagged) return;
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(this, () -> {
            Player p = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);
            if (p != null && p.isOnline()) flagPlayer(p, "AutoClicker-ML", evidence);
        });
    }

    @Override
    public void onDisable() {
        shutdownMlEngine();                     // NUEVO
        if (recorder != null) recorder.close(); // NUEVO
        getLogger().info("Proyecto_CiberPlugin DESACTIVADO.");
    }

    @EventHandler
    public void onPlayerQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        UUID uuid = event.getPlayer().getUniqueId();
        recorder.stopSession(uuid); // NUEVO
        clickCleaners.remove(uuid);
        if (mlDetector != null) mlDetector.remove(uuid);
        lastBlockBreakTime.remove(uuid);
        violationScores.remove(uuid);
        lastAlertTime.remove(uuid);
    }

    // MOTOR DE DECISION Y SCORING
    private void flagPlayer(Player player, String cheatType, String evidence) {
        // BASTIONADO: Control de Accesos (RBAC) - Ignorar si tiene permiso
        if (player.hasPermission("ciberplugin.bypass")) return;

        // NUEVO: no sancionar mientras se graba una sesion
        if (recorder.isRecording(player.getUniqueId())) return;

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        int score = violationScores.getOrDefault(uuid, 0) + 1;
        violationScores.put(uuid, score);

        // Control de Spam de alertas
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now - lastAlertTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L) > 1000) {
            lastAlertTime.put(uuid, now);

            String alertMsg = String.format("§8[§c!§8] §c%s §7fallo §e%s §8[VL: %d] §7(%s)",
                    player.getName(), cheatType, score, evidence);

            for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
                if (p.hasPermission("ciberplugin.admin")) {
                    p.sendMessage(alertMsg);
                }
            }
        }

        logViolationAsync(player, cheatType, evidence, score);

        if (score >= maxViolations) {
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(this, () -> {
                player.kickPlayer("§cHas sido desconectado.\n§7Motivo: Comportamiento anomalo detectado.");
                violationScores.remove(uuid);
            });
        }
    }

    private void logViolationAsync(Player player, String cheatType, String evidence, int score) {
        // Seudonimo calculado en el hilo principal (no se accede al objeto Player desde el hilo asincrono)
        final String actor = pseudonym(player.getUniqueId());
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(this, () -> {
            try {
                File logFile = new File(getDataFolder(), "auditoria.log");
                if (!logFile.exists()) {
                    logFile.getParentFile().mkdirs();
                    logFile.createNewFile();
                }
                try (PrintWriter out = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(logFile, true))) {
                    String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"));

                    // BASTIONADO: Privacidad por Diseno (seudonimo HMAC-SHA256 con clave, no UUID recortado)
                    out.printf("[%s] ALERT | Actor: %s | Cheat: %s | VL: %d | Evidencia: %s%n",
                            timestamp, actor, cheatType, score, evidence);
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                getLogger().warning("Error escribiendo auditoria forense: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    // MODO SHADOW: se registra cada ventana puntuada por el modelo sin sumar VL (validacion en produccion)
    private void logMlShadowAsync(UUID uuid, double score, boolean flagged, String evidence) {
        String actor = pseudonym(uuid);
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(this, () -> {
            try {
                File f = new File(getDataFolder(), "ml_shadow.csv");
                boolean isNew = !f.exists();
                if (isNew) {
                    f.getParentFile().mkdirs();
                    f.createNewFile();
                }
                try (PrintWriter out = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(f, true))) {
                    if (isNew) out.println("timestamp,actor,score,threshold,would_flag,evidence");
                    String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss"));
                    out.printf(Locale.ROOT, "%s,%s,%.4f,%.4f,%b,\"%s\"%n", timestamp, actor,
                            score, mlThreshold, flagged, evidence.replace("\"", "'"));
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                getLogger().warning("Error escribiendo ml_shadow.csv: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    // Seudonimo del jugador para los registros (HMAC-SHA256 con la clave secreta del servidor)
    private String pseudonym(UUID uuid) {
        return pseudonymizer.pseudonym(uuid);
    }

    // EXPORTACIÓN PARA MACHINE LEARNING
    private void exportTelemetryForML(UUID uuid, long timeBetweenClicks) {
        final String actor = pseudonym(uuid);
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(this, () -> {
            try {
                File mlFile = new File(getDataFolder(), "telemetria_ml.csv");
                boolean isNew = !mlFile.exists();
                if (isNew) {
                    mlFile.getParentFile().mkdirs();
                    mlFile.createNewFile();
                }
                try (PrintWriter out = new PrintWriter(new FileWriter(mlFile, true))) {
                    if (isNew) out.println("timestamp,uuid_ofuscado,delay_ms,label");

                    String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss"));
                    out.printf("%s,%s,%d,unknown%n", timestamp, actor, timeBetweenClicks);
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
            }
        });
    }

    // DETECCIONES DE MOVIMIENTO (SPEED / FLY)
    @EventHandler
    public void onPlayerMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        Location from = event.getFrom();
        Location to = event.getTo();

        if (to == null) return;

        // NUEVO: registrar movimiento (ignora giros de camara)
        double dx = to.getX() - from.getX();
        double dy = to.getY() - from.getY();
        double dz = to.getZ() - from.getZ();
        if (dx != 0 || dy != 0 || dz != 0) {
            recorder.logMove(player, dx, dy, dz);
        }

        double horizontalDistance = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(to.getX() - from.getX(), 2) + Math.pow(to.getZ() - from.getZ(), 2));
        double verticalDistance = Math.abs(to.getY() - from.getY());

        if (horizontalDistance > maxHorizontalDist) {
            flagPlayer(player, "Speed/Movement", "Distancia H: " + String.format("%.2f", horizontalDistance));
        }

        if (!player.isOnGround() && verticalDistance > 1.0) {
            flagPlayer(player, "Fly", "Distancia V: " + String.format("%.2f", verticalDistance));
        }

        Block block = to.getBlock();
        if (block.getType().isSolid()) {
            flagPlayer(player, "No-Clip", "Atraveso bloque: " + block.getType().name());
        }
    }

    // NUEVA DETECCIÓN DE COMBATE (REACH / KILLAURA)
    @EventHandler
    public void onPlayerAttack(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (event.getDamager() instanceof Player) {
            Player attacker = (Player) event.getDamager();
            Location attackerLoc = attacker.getLocation();
            Location victimLoc = event.getEntity().getLocation();

            double distance = attackerLoc.distance(victimLoc);

            if (distance > maxCombatReach) {
                flagPlayer(attacker, "Reach/KillAura", "Distancia ataque: " + String.format("%.2f", distance) + " bloques");
            }
        }
    }

    // DETECCIONES DE AUTOMATIZACIÓN (CLICS)
    // CORREGIDO: un clic fisico genera hasta 3 eventos en ~5 ms (LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK, LEFT_CLICK_AIR y el
    // swing), y medir intervalos entre eventos de interaccion daba falsos positivos. Ahora:
    //  - el clic es el swing del brazo (onSwing), de-duplicado a 15 ms;
    //  - LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK y las roturas solo marcan una excavacion, para no evaluar los swings que el
    //    cliente genera cada tick mientras se mantiene pulsado el boton (minar).
    @EventHandler
    public void onPlayerInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        Action action = event.getAction();
        if (action != Action.LEFT_CLICK_AIR && action != Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK) return;
        if (event.getHand() != EquipmentSlot.HAND) return;

        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        recorder.logClick(player, action == Action.LEFT_CLICK_AIR ? "interact_air" : "interact_block");

        if (action == Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK) {
            cleanerOf(player.getUniqueId()).onDigMarker(nowMs());
        }
    }

    // El swing del brazo es la fuente del clic
    @EventHandler
    public void onSwing(PlayerAnimationEvent event) {
        if (event.getAnimationType() != PlayerAnimationType.ARM_SWING) return;
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        recorder.logClick(player, "swing");

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        ClickStreamCleaner.Swing swing = cleanerOf(uuid).onSwing(nowMs());
        if (swing == null) return;                                   // duplicado
        if (mlDetector != null) mlDetector.onAccepted(uuid, swing);  // el modelo ve tambien los swings "held"
        if (swing.held || Double.isNaN(swing.ivMs)) return;          // minando / primer clic
        if (clickEngine.equals("ml")) return;                        // en modo ml el umbral queda de respaldo

        long diff = (long) Math.floor(swing.ivMs);
        exportTelemetryForML(uuid, diff);

        if (diff > 0 && diff < (1000 / maxClicksPerSecond)) {
            flagPlayer(player, "AutoClicker", "Click delay: " + diff + "ms");
        }
    }

    private ClickStreamCleaner cleanerOf(UUID uuid) {
        return clickCleaners.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> new ClickStreamCleaner());
    }

    private static double nowMs() {
        return System.nanoTime() / 1_000_000.0;
    }

    @EventHandler
    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long current = System.currentTimeMillis();

        recorder.logBreak(player); // NUEVO
        cleanerOf(uuid).onDigMarker(nowMs()); // la rotura tambien abre/continua una excavacion

        if (lastBlockBreakTime.containsKey(uuid)) {
            long last = lastBlockBreakTime.get(uuid);
            long diff = current - last;

            if (diff < minBreakDelay) {
                flagPlayer(player, "FastBreak", "Break delay: " + diff + "ms");
            }
        }
        lastBlockBreakTime.put(uuid, current);
    }

    public void reloadPluginConfig() {
        reloadConfig();
        loadConfigValues();
        loadPseudonymizer();
        initMlEngine();
    }
}
```

`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/Pseudonymizer.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import javax.crypto.Mac;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Seudonimizacion con clave: HMAC-SHA256(clave_secreta, UUID).
 *
 * A diferencia de recortar el UUID (que deja 32 bits del identificador real y es reversible buscando
 * el prefijo entre los UUID conocidos), sin la clave no se puede volver al UUID ni enlazar con otros
 * sistemas; con la clave el mismo jugador recibe siempre el mismo seudonimo (trazabilidad forense).
 * Es seudonimizacion, no anonimizacion: quien tenga la clave puede recalcular el seudonimo.
 */
public final class Pseudonymizer {

    public static final int MIN_KEY_BYTES = 16;
    private static final int HEX_CHARS = 16; // 64 bits: colisiones despreciables en la poblacion de un servidor

    private final byte[] key;
    private final ThreadLocal<Mac> mac; // Mac no es thread-safe y la auditoria se escribe en hilos asincronos

    public Pseudonymizer(byte[] key) {
        if (key == null || key.length < MIN_KEY_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("La clave HMAC debe tener al menos " + MIN_KEY_BYTES + " bytes");
        }
        this.key = key.clone();
        this.mac = ThreadLocal.withInitial(this::newMac);
    }

    /** Clave aleatoria de 32 bytes en Base64 (para guardar en config.yml). */
    public static String generateKey() {
        byte[] k = new byte[32];
        new SecureRandom().nextBytes(k);
        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(k);
    }

    /** Decodifica la clave de config.yml; null si falta o no es valida. */
    public static byte[] decodeKey(String b64) {
        if (b64 == null || b64.isBlank()) return null;
        try {
            byte[] k = Base64.getDecoder().decode(b64.trim());
            return k.length >= MIN_KEY_BYTES ? k : null;
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public String pseudonym(UUID uuid) {
        return "p-" + hex(hmac(uuid.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))).substring(0, HEX_CHARS);
    }

    byte[] hmac(byte[] data) {
        return mac.get().doFinal(data);
    }

    private Mac newMac() {
        try {
            Mac m = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256");
            m.init(new SecretKeySpec(key, "HmacSHA256"));
            return m;
        } catch (GeneralSecurityException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("HmacSHA256 no disponible", e);
        }
    }

    static String hex(byte[] b) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(b.length * 2);
        for (byte x : b) sb.append(Character.forDigit((x >> 4) & 0xF, 16)).append(Character.forDigit(x & 0xF, 16));
        return sb.toString();
    }
}

```

`plugin/src/main/java/org/example/SessionRecorder.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;

import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.BlockingQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

public class SessionRecorder {

    private static final Pattern SAFE = Pattern.compile("[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,32}");
    private static final int QUEUE_CAPACITY = 50_000;

    // índices: 0 clicks, 1 moves, 2 breaks, 3 sessions
    private static final String[] NAMES = {"clicks", "moves", "breaks", "sessions"};
    private static final String[] HEADERS = {
            "session_id,t_ns,source,label",
            "session_id,t_ns,dx,dy,dz,on_ground,tps,ping,label",
            "session_id,t_ns,label",
            "session_id,participant,label,start_utc,end_utc,duration_s,events_dropped"
    };

    public static final class Session {
        public final String sessionId;
        public final String participant;
        public final String label;
        final long startNanos = System.nanoTime();
        final Instant startUtc = Instant.now();
        final AtomicLong dropped = new AtomicLong();

        Session(String sessionId, String participant, String label) {
            this.sessionId = sessionId;
            this.participant = participant;
            this.label = label;
        }
    }

    private record Row(int file, String line) {}

    private final Proyecto_ciberPlugin plugin;
    private final Map<UUID, Session> sessions = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final BlockingQueue<Row> queue = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(QUEUE_CAPACITY);
    private volatile boolean running;
    private volatile double tps = 20.0;
    private long lastTickCheck = System.nanoTime();
    private Thread writer;
    private BukkitTask tpsTask;

    public SessionRecorder(Proyecto_ciberPlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    public static boolean isSafe(String s) {
        return SAFE.matcher(s).matches();
    }

    // ---------- ciclo de vida ----------
    public void open() {
        running = true;
        writer = new Thread(this::writeLoop, "CiberPlugin-DatasetWriter");
        writer.setDaemon(true);
        writer.start();

        // TPS medido a mano: una tarea cada 20 ticks deberia tardar 1 s a 20 TPS
        lastTickCheck = System.nanoTime();
        tpsTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(plugin, () -> {
            long now = System.nanoTime();
            double elapsedS = (now - lastTickCheck) / 1_000_000_000.0;
            lastTickCheck = now;
            if (elapsedS > 0) tps = Math.min(20.0, 20.0 / elapsedS);
        }, 20L, 20L);
    }

    public void close() {
        for (UUID id : sessions.keySet()) stopSession(id);
        if (tpsTask != null) tpsTask.cancel();
        running = false;
        try {
            if (writer != null) writer.join(3000);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }

    // ---------- sesiones ----------
    public Session startSession(Player p, String label, String participant) {
        if (!isSafe(label) || !isSafe(participant)) return null;
        String id = UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8);
        Session s = new Session(id, participant, label);
        return sessions.putIfAbsent(p.getUniqueId(), s) == null ? s : null;
    }

    public Session stopSession(UUID playerId) {
        Session s = sessions.remove(playerId);
        if (s == null) return null;
        long durationS = (System.nanoTime() - s.startNanos) / 1_000_000_000L;
        enqueue(s, 3, String.join(",", s.sessionId, s.participant, s.label,
                s.startUtc.toString(), Instant.now().toString(),
                Long.toString(durationS), Long.toString(s.dropped.get())));
        return s;
    }

    public boolean isRecording(UUID playerId) {
        return sessions.containsKey(playerId);
    }

    // ---------- registro de eventos (se llaman desde el hilo principal) ----------
    public void logClick(Player p, String source) {
        Session s = sessions.get(p.getUniqueId());
        if (s == null) return;
        enqueue(s, 0, s.sessionId + "," + (System.nanoTime() - s.startNanos) + "," + source + "," + s.label);
    }

    public void logMove(Player p, double dx, double dy, double dz) {
        Session s = sessions.get(p.getUniqueId());
        if (s == null) return;
        long t = System.nanoTime() - s.startNanos;
        // Locale.ROOT: con locale español %.2f escribiría comas y rompería el CSV
        enqueue(s, 1, String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%s,%d,%.4f,%.4f,%.4f,%d,%.2f,%d,%s",
                s.sessionId, t, dx, dy, dz, p.isOnGround() ? 1 : 0, tps, p.getPing(), s.label));
    }

    public void logBreak(Player p) {
        Session s = sessions.get(p.getUniqueId());
        if (s == null) return;
        enqueue(s, 2, s.sessionId + "," + (System.nanoTime() - s.startNanos) + "," + s.label);
    }

    private void enqueue(Session s, int file, String line) {
        // offer() no bloquea: si la cola está llena se descarta y se cuenta (backpressure)
        if (!queue.offer(new Row(file, line))) s.dropped.incrementAndGet();
    }

    // ---------- hilo escritor ----------
    private void writeLoop() {
        BufferedWriter[] out = new BufferedWriter[NAMES.length];
        try {
            Path dir = plugin.getDataFolder().toPath().resolve("dataset");
            Files.createDirectories(dir);
            for (int i = 0; i < NAMES.length; i++) {
                Path p = dir.resolve(NAMES[i] + ".csv");
                boolean isNew = !Files.exists(p) || Files.size(p) == 0;
                out[i] = Files.newBufferedWriter(p, StandardCharsets.UTF_8,
                        StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND);
                if (isNew) {
                    out[i].write(HEADERS[i]);
                    out[i].newLine();
                }
            }
            int sinceFlush = 0;
            while (running || !queue.isEmpty()) {
                Row r = queue.poll(500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
                if (r == null) {
                    flushAll(out);
                    sinceFlush = 0;
                    continue;
                }
                out[r.file()].write(r.line());
                out[r.file()].newLine();
                if (++sinceFlush >= 500) {
                    flushAll(out);
                    sinceFlush = 0;
                }
            }
            flushAll(out);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            plugin.getLogger().warning("Error escribiendo dataset: " + e.getMessage());
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        } finally {
            for (BufferedWriter w : out) {
                if (w != null) {
                    try { w.close(); } catch (IOException ignored) { }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void flushAll(BufferedWriter[] out) throws IOException {
        for (BufferedWriter w : out) if (w != null) w.flush();
    }
}
```

`plugin/src/main/resources/config.yml`:

```yml
# Configuración de Bastionado - Proyecto CiberPlugin
sanciones:
  max_violations_before_kick: 15

deteccion:
  autoclicker:
    max_clicks_per_second: 15
    # Motor de deteccion de AutoClicker:
    #   threshold -> detector original por umbral (por defecto)
    #   shadow    -> umbral activo + el modelo ML solo se registra en ml_shadow.csv (sin VL)
    #   ml        -> el modelo ML suma VL; si el modelo no carga se vuelve al umbral
    # El modelo incluido es una DEMO entrenada con datos sinteticos: no usar 'ml' sin validarlo antes.
    engine: threshold
    # 0 = umbral calibrado del modelo (FPR 1% en humanos de calibracion); si no, valor en [0.5, 0.9999]
    ml_threshold: 0
  fastbreak:
    min_delay_ms: 450
  speed:
    max_horizontal_distance: 0.1

privacidad:
  # Clave secreta para seudonimizar jugadores en auditoria.log, telemetria_ml.csv y ml_shadow.csv
  # (HMAC-SHA256 del UUID). Se genera automaticamente en el primer arranque si esta vacia.
  # NO la compartas ni la subas a git: con ella se pueden recalcular los seudonimos.
  # Cambiarla hace que los seudonimos nuevos no se puedan enlazar con los antiguos.
  hmac_key: ""

```

`plugin/src/test/java/org/example/ClickStreamCleanerTest.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class ClickStreamCleanerTest {

    @Test
    void duplicateSwingsAreMergedIntoLastAccepted() {
        ClickStreamCleaner c = new ClickStreamCleaner();
        assertNotNull(c.onSwing(0));
        assertNull(c.onSwing(10));          // < 15 ms -> duplicate
        ClickStreamCleaner.Swing s = c.onSwing(20);
        assertNotNull(s);
        assertEquals(20.0, s.ivMs, 1e-9);
        assertNull(c.onSwing(31));
        assertEquals(1, s.nDup);
    }

    @Test
    void instantBreakThenHeldSwingsAreHeld() {
        // pattern seen in the real data: interact_block, break, the click's swing, then one swing per tick
        ClickStreamCleaner c = new ClickStreamCleaner();
        c.onDigMarker(0.0);
        c.onDigMarker(0.2);
        assertFalse(c.onSwing(0.6).held);   // the press itself is a real click
        assertTrue(c.onSwing(50.1).held);
        assertTrue(c.onSwing(105.4).held);
        c.onDigMarker(657.0);
        assertFalse(c.onSwing(657.6).held); // new press after release
        assertTrue(c.onSwing(705.0).held);
    }

    @Test
    void fastClicksInAirAreNeverHeld() {
        ClickStreamCleaner c = new ClickStreamCleaner();
        for (int k = 0; k < 20; k++) {
            ClickStreamCleaner.Swing s = c.onSwing(50.0 * k);
            assertFalse(s.held);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void releaseEndsHeldRun() {
        ClickStreamCleaner c = new ClickStreamCleaner();
        c.onDigMarker(1000);
        c.onSwing(1002);
        for (int k = 1; k < 7; k++) assertTrue(c.onSwing(1002 + 50.0 * k).held);
        assertFalse(c.onSwing(1802).held);  // 500 ms later: released and clicked again
        assertFalse(c.onSwing(1850).held);  // no dig marker -> not held
    }
}
```

`plugin/src/test/java/org/example/MlParityTest.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import com.google.gson.JsonArray;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Paridad Java <-> Python. click_features_fixture.json lo genera ml/src/export_onnx.py a partir de
 * las implementaciones de referencia en Python (data.clean_click_stream y features.window_features).
 */
class MlParityTest {

    private static JsonObject fixture() throws Exception {
        try (InputStream in = Objects.requireNonNull(
                MlParityTest.class.getResourceAsStream("/click_features_fixture.json"), "fixture missing")) {
            return JsonParser.parseReader(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).getAsJsonObject();
        }
    }

    @Test
    void featureOrderMatchesPython() throws Exception {
        JsonArray f = fixture().getAsJsonArray("features");
        assertEquals(ClickFeatureExtractor.FEATURES.length, f.size());
        for (int i = 0; i < f.size(); i++) assertEquals(f.get(i).getAsString(), ClickFeatureExtractor.FEATURES[i]);
    }

    @Test
    void cleanerAndFeaturesMatchPython() throws Exception {
        for (JsonElement ce : fixture().getAsJsonArray("cases")) {
            JsonObject c = ce.getAsJsonObject();
            String name = c.get("name").getAsString();
            // merge click events and breaks in time order (same tie rule as Python)
            List<double[]> ev = new ArrayList<>(); // {t, kind} kind: 0 interact_block, 1 interact_air, 2 break, 3 swing
            for (JsonElement e : c.getAsJsonArray("events")) {
                JsonArray a = e.getAsJsonArray();
                String src = a.get(1).getAsString();
                int kind = src.equals("interact_block") ? 0 : src.equals("interact_air") ? 1 : 3;
                ev.add(new double[]{a.get(0).getAsDouble(), kind});
            }
            for (JsonElement b : c.getAsJsonArray("breaks")) ev.add(new double[]{b.getAsDouble(), 2});
            // Python converts to integer ns first; do the same so ties/rounding are identical
            for (double[] e : ev) e[0] = ((long) (e[0] * 1e6)) / 1e6;
            ev.sort((x, y) -> x[0] != y[0] ? Double.compare(x[0], y[0]) : Double.compare(x[1], y[1]));

            ClickStreamCleaner cl = new ClickStreamCleaner();
            List<ClickStreamCleaner.Swing> acc = new ArrayList<>();
            for (double[] e : ev) {
                if (e[1] == 0 || e[1] == 2) cl.onDigMarker(e[0]);
                else if (e[1] == 3) {
                    ClickStreamCleaner.Swing s = cl.onSwing(e[0]);
                    if (s != null) acc.add(s);
                }
            }
            JsonArray expT = c.getAsJsonArray("accepted_t_ms");
            JsonArray expHeld = c.getAsJsonArray("held");
            JsonArray expDup = c.getAsJsonArray("n_dup");
            assertEquals(expT.size(), acc.size(), name + ": accepted swings");
            double[] t = new double[acc.size()];
            boolean[] held = new boolean[acc.size()];
            int[] dup = new int[acc.size()];
            for (int i = 0; i < acc.size(); i++) {
                assertEquals(expT.get(i).getAsDouble(), acc.get(i).tMs, 1e-6, name + " t[" + i + "]");
                assertEquals(expHeld.get(i).getAsBoolean(), acc.get(i).held, name + " held[" + i + "]");
                assertEquals(expDup.get(i).getAsInt(), acc.get(i).nDup, name + " dup[" + i + "]");
                t[i] = acc.get(i).tMs;
                held[i] = acc.get(i).held;
                dup[i] = acc.get(i).nDup;
            }
            ClickFeatureExtractor.Result r = ClickFeatureExtractor.compute(t, held, dup);
            JsonObject ef = c.getAsJsonObject("features");
            assertEquals(ef.get("valid").getAsBoolean(), r.valid, name + " valid");
            assertEquals(ef.get("n_active").getAsInt(), r.nActive, name + " n_active");
            assertEquals(ef.get("held_frac").getAsDouble(), r.heldFrac, 1e-9, name + " held_frac");
            for (String f : ClickFeatureExtractor.FEATURES) {
                JsonElement v = ef.get(f);
                if (v == null || v.isJsonNull()) {
                    assertTrue(Double.isNaN(r.get(f)), name + " " + f + " should be NaN");
                } else {
                    double exp = v.getAsDouble();
                    assertEquals(exp, r.get(f), 1e-6 * Math.max(1.0, Math.abs(exp)), name + " " + f);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void onnxModelMatchesPythonScores() throws Exception {
        Path onnx = Path.of("src/main/resources/models/click_model.onnx");
        Path meta = Path.of("src/main/resources/models/click_model.json");
        assertTrue(Files.exists(onnx), "run ml/src/export_onnx.py first");
        try (ClickModel m = ClickModel.load(onnx, meta)) {
            for (JsonElement se : fixture().getAsJsonArray("model_samples")) {
                JsonObject s = se.getAsJsonObject();
                JsonArray xa = s.getAsJsonArray("x");
                float[] x = new float[xa.size()];
                for (int i = 0; i < x.length; i++) x[i] = xa.get(i).getAsFloat();
                assertEquals(s.get("score").getAsDouble(), m.score(x), 1e-5);
            }
            assertTrue(m.threshold > 0 && m.threshold < 1);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void detectorScoresWindowsOffTheCallerThread() throws Exception {
        List<String> threads = new ArrayList<>();
        java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch done = new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
        MlClickDetector d = new MlClickDetector(x -> 0.5f, (p, s, f) -> {
            threads.add(Thread.currentThread().getName());
            done.countDown();
        });
        java.util.UUID u = java.util.UUID.randomUUID();
        ClickStreamCleaner c = new ClickStreamCleaner();
        for (int i = 0; i <= MlClickDetector.WINDOW; i++) {
            d.onAccepted(u, c.onSwing(100.0 * i + (i % 3) * 7));
        }
        assertTrue(done.await(5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS));
        assertEquals("CiberPlugin-ML", threads.get(0));
        d.shutdown();
    }
}

```

`plugin/src/test/java/org/example/PseudonymizerTest.java`:

```java
package org.example;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.UUID;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class PseudonymizerTest {

    @Test
    void hmacMatchesRfc4231TestCase1() {
        // RFC 4231 test case 1: key = 20 x 0x0b, data = "Hi There"
        byte[] key = new byte[20];
        Arrays.fill(key, (byte) 0x0b);
        Pseudonymizer p = new Pseudonymizer(key);
        String mac = Pseudonymizer.hex(p.hmac("Hi There".getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)));
        assertEquals("b0344c61d8db38535ca8afceaf0bf12b881dc200c9833da726e9376c2e32cff7", mac);
    }

    @Test
    void stableKeyedAndNotATruncatedUuid() {
        UUID u = UUID.fromString("123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000");
        Pseudonymizer a = new Pseudonymizer(Pseudonymizer.decodeKey(Pseudonymizer.generateKey()));
        Pseudonymizer b = new Pseudonymizer(Pseudonymizer.decodeKey(Pseudonymizer.generateKey()));
        assertEquals(a.pseudonym(u), a.pseudonym(u));
        assertNotEquals(a.pseudonym(u), b.pseudonym(u));
        assertTrue(a.pseudonym(u).matches("p-[0-9a-f]{16}"));
        assertFalse(a.pseudonym(u).contains("123e4567"));
    }

    @Test
    void invalidKeysAreRejected() {
        assertNull(Pseudonymizer.decodeKey(""));
        assertNull(Pseudonymizer.decodeKey("not base64 !!"));
        assertNull(Pseudonymizer.decodeKey("c2hvcnQ="));            // "short": < 16 bytes
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new Pseudonymizer(new byte[8]));
    }
}
```
